### [1] [Architecturally Significant MLOps Guidelines for ML Model Integration and Deployment: a Gray Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06535)
*Faezeh Amou Najafabad,Markus Haug,Keerthiga Rajenthiram,Justus Bogner,Ilias Gerostathopoulos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对103个网络来源的灰色文献综述，总结了25条MLOps系统中机器学习模型集成与部署的架构指导原则，分为五大类别，旨在为研究人员和实践者提供实践导向的架构参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLOps项目因缺乏统一的架构指导而常以临时方式开展，社区需要一份综合性的参考来指导MLOps系统的架构设计，特别是关于ML模型的集成与部署。

Method: 对103个网络来源进行灰色文献综述，分析MLOps模型集成与部署的实践知识，随后采用主题分析将这些实践综合为推荐指南。

Result: 贡献了25条具有架构重要性的MLOps指南，涵盖模型集成与部署，分为五个类别，并描述了它们对整体系统架构的影响。

Conclusion: 研究结果为MLOps中ML模型集成与部署提供了实践导向的指南概览，可支持研究人员和实践者更好地设计其MLOps系统架构。

Abstract: Context. Despite the growing adoption of Machine Learning Operations (MLOps), teams often approach MLOps projects in an ad hoc manner due to the lack of consolidated architectural guidance. The community would benefit from a reference that synthesizes knowledge to inform the architectural design of MLOps systems, especially regarding the integration and deployment of ML models. Objective. In response, our goal is to provide a comprehensive overview of architecturally significant guidelines for the integration and deployment of ML models in MLOps systems. Method. We conduct a gray literature review of 103 web sources to analyze state-of-practice knowledge on MLOps model integration and deployment. We then apply thematic analysis to synthesize these practices into recommended guidelines. Results. We contribute a collection of 25 architecturally significant MLOps guidelines for model integration and deployment, organized into five categories, and describe their impact on the overall system architecture. Conclusion. Our results serve as an overview of state-of-practice MLOps guidelines to support researchers and practitioners with the integration and deployment of ML models in their MLOps systems.

</details>


### [2] [Queen-Bee Agents: A BeeSpec-Centered Architecture for Governed Enterprise MCP Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06545)
*Dutao Zhang,Liaotian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Queen-Bee是一种受治理的多智能体架构，通过Queen控制平面进行能力检索、任务规划和结构化BeeSpec生成，由Bee智能体在受限工具访问下执行。在59项企业任务上，检索驱动变体实现了0.964的任务成功率、零治理失败，并显著优于静态基线和单智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体系统需要将大语言模型连接到私有工具、内部知识和MCP接口，但原始任务能力不足以满足策略执行、租户隔离和操作边界约束等治理需求。

Method: 提出Queen-Bee架构：Queen控制平面负责能力检索、任务规划和生成结构化BeeSpec；Bee智能体在受限工具访问下执行Spec。实现包括租户级MCP连接器、审计支持的执行时治理、检索驱动的弱孵化机制和多种配置后端。

Result: 在59项企业风格任务上，检索驱动Queen-Bee变体任务成功率为0.964，零治理失败，执行质量显著优于静态Queen-Bee基线和宽松单智能体基线。多Bee化学工作流展示了明确的审批门控和基于上游证据的top-3候选排序。

Conclusion: 企业智能体平台不仅应评估任务能力，还应评估受治理的配置、隔离行为、范围化执行质量和工件感知的工作流协调能力。混合检索和LLM引导的配置后端虽可行，但在当前小型高结构化能力注册表上未超越轻量级结构化检索器。

Abstract: Enterprise agent systems increasingly need to connect large language models to private tools, internal knowledge, and Model Context Protocol (MCP) interfaces. In this setting, raw task capability is insufficient: organizations also require policy enforcement, tenant-scoped isolation, and execution that remains within explicit operational boundaries. We present Queen-Bee, a governed multi-agent architecture in which a Queen control plane retrieves capabilities, plans task-scoped execution, and compiles a structured BeeSpec that is executed by specialized Bee agents under constrained tool access. We implement a working prototype with tenant-scoped MCP connectors, audit-backed execution-time governance, retrieval-driven weak incubation, and multiple provisioning backends. We evaluate the system on 59 enterprise-style tasks spanning governance-sensitive requests, retrieval-driven provisioning, scoped local execution, and chemistry workflow integration. The retrieval-driven Queen-Bee variant achieves a task success rate of 0.964, zero governance failures, and substantially better scoped execution quality than both a static Queen-Bee baseline and a permissive single-agent baseline. We further show a multi-Bee chemistry workflow with explicit approval gating and a concrete top-3 shortlist grounded in real upstream evidence and screening artifacts. Additional comparisons with hybrid retrieval and LLM-guided provisioning show that richer provisioning backends are viable but do not outperform the lightweight structured retriever on the current small, highly structured capability registry. The results provide prototype-level systems evidence rather than a production deployment study, and suggest that enterprise agent platforms should be evaluated not only by capability, but also by governed provisioning, isolation behavior, scoped execution quality, and artifact-aware workflow coordination.

</details>


### [3] [AI-Driven Test Case Generation from Natural Language Requirements: A Survey of Techniques and Research Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06563)
*Orimoloye Folorunsho,Hassan Reza*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该综述系统分析了2000-2025年间21项基于AI/NLP从自然语言需求生成测试用例的研究，发现现有方法未能同时满足自动化、歧义处理、领域适用性、可追溯性、评估彻底性和幻觉控制六大质量维度，并提出了四项研究指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件测试耗时且昂贵，基于需求的测试生成可从需求工件早期导出测试用例，但自然语言固有的歧义和不精确性使得直接生成面临挑战。AI、NLP和LLM的进步使自动化此流程日益可行，但也引入了幻觉、可追溯性降低和评估不一致等新风险。

Method: 遵循Kitchenham和Charters的系统综述指南，在2000-2025年间多个主要学术数据库进行检索，经严格纳入标准筛选后确定21篇主要研究。将文献组织为三个演化时代进行综合分析。

Result: 发现现有方法无法同时满足六大质量维度：自动化、歧义处理、领域适用性、可追溯性、评估彻底性和幻觉控制。提出了基于三个时代的演化综合、六维度差距分析（表明无方法完全满足所有维度）以及四项可操作研究指南。

Conclusion: 该综述贡献了AI测试生成的三个时代演化综合、六维差距分析及四项研究指南，分别针对幻觉、可追溯性、复杂度敏感性和合规性，为未来研究方向提供了系统框架。

Abstract: Software testing is critical for verifying that systems meet specified requirements, yet remains among the most time-consuming and expensive activities in development. Requirements-based test generation allows test cases to be derived early from requirements artifacts, but generating them directly from natural language is challenging due to inherent ambiguity and imprecision. Recent advances in AI, natural language processing (NLP), and large language models (LLMs) have made automating this pipeline increasingly feasible, while introducing new risks including hallucination, reduced traceability, and inconsistent evaluation. This survey addresses four research questions: what AI and NLP techniques have been proposed for generating test cases from natural language requirements; what tools and frameworks support these approaches; how generated test cases are evaluated; and what research gaps remain. Following Kitchenham and Charters' systematic review guidelines, we searched major scholarly databases spanning 2000-2025 and, after applying strict inclusion criteria, identified 21 primary studies. The literature is organized into three evolutionary eras, revealing that no existing approach simultaneously satisfies six key quality dimensions: automation, ambiguity handling, domain applicability, traceability, evaluation thoroughness, and hallucination control. The survey makes three main contributions: a three-era evolutionary synthesis of AI-based test generation; a six-criteria gap analysis showing no current approach fully addresses all quality dimensions; and four actionable research guidelines targeting hallucination, traceability, complexity sensitivity, and compliance.

</details>


### [4] [NTILC: Neural Tool Invocation via Learned Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06566)
*Andrew Krikorian,Yayuan Li,Jason J. Corso*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: NTILC是一个神经工具选择与调用框架，通过将工具规格映射到共享嵌入空间进行潜在检索，替代传统的上下文内工具查找，从而减少95%以上的上下文窗口消耗和高达74%的推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具调用语言模型将全部工具规格直接放入提示中，导致成本随工具注册表大小线性增长，消耗上下文预算、增加延迟并降低选择准确性，尤其是无关工具的干扰问题。

Method: 提出NTILC框架，将用户意图和工具规格映射到共享嵌入空间，通过外部检索而非上下文查找选择工具；语言模型仅基于选中的工具模式进行参数生成。核心是签名感知复合目标函数，结合Circle Loss和Functional Margin Loss，增强语义相似性并利用工具签名约束（参数模式、类型兼容性、返回类型）区分语义相似但执行签名不兼容的工具。

Result: 在公开工具选择和函数调用数据集上，相比长上下文ICT基线，NTILC减少了95%以上的上下文窗口消耗，推理延迟降低高达74%。

Conclusion: NTILC通过潜在检索替代上下文内工具查找，显著降低了上下文消耗和延迟，同时提升了工具选择的准确性。

Abstract: Agentic tool-calling language models depend on large registries of callable APIs, functions, and local actions. Placing full tool specifications directly in the prompt incurs a cost that scales linearly with the size of the tool registry, rapidly consuming the context budget. As the registry grows, this leads to higher latency and degrades selection accuracy, particularly due to interference from irrelevant tools. We overcome these limitations by introducing NTILC, a neural tool selection and invocation framework that replaces in-context registry look-up with learned latent retrieval. NTILC maps both user intent and tool specifications into a shared embedding space, enabling tool selection via external retrieval rather than in-context lookup. The language model is conditioned only on the selected tool schema, allowing for precise, constrained argument generation. Central to our approach is a signature-aware composite objective, which augments semantic similarity with constraints derived from tool signatures (e.g., argument schema, type compatibility, and return types). By combining Circle Loss with a Functional Margin Loss, the model enforces separation between tools that are semantically similar but incompatible under their execution signatures. We evaluate NTILC on public tool-selection and function-calling datasets and report context token usage, retrieval accuracy, and selection latency metrics. Across these settings, NTILC reduces context window consumption by over 95% and inference latency by up to 74% compared to long-context ICT baselines.

</details>


### [5] [QBugLM: An Agentic Benchmarking Framework for LLM-based Quantum Software Debugging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07314)
*An B. B. Pham,Hoa T. Nguyen,Muhammad Usman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出QBugLM，一个多智能体框架，用于自动化量子软件调试流水线（从注入bug到检测修复再到仿真验证），并通过案例研究评估LLM在量子程序调试中的表现，发现迭代反馈和结构化提示策略是关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件bug常产生静默错误输出而非显式报错，传统技术难以检测和修复；LLM在经典软件工程中表现强劲，但其在量子代码调试方面的能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出QBugLM多智能体框架，包含三个模块：基于分类学的bug注入、基于LLM的检测与修复（支持多种提示策略）、基于仿真的验证。该框架支持框架无关的OpenQASM 3.0程序。

Result: 单次重试将Pass@1从低于25%提升至高于80%；在固定资源约束下，简单的结构化提示策略表现优于Chain-of-Thought和ReAct等推理型提示策略。

Conclusion: 迭代反馈对量子程序调试至关重要；结构化提示对推理能力强的模型更为高效；该工作为自动化量子软件修复的LLM能力基准测试奠定了基础。

Abstract: Quantum software bugs often yield silent, incorrect outputs rather than explicit errors, making them particularly difficult to detect and repair with conventional techniques. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on classical software engineering tasks, their ability to debug quantum code remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose QBugLM, a multi-agent framework that automates the quantum software debugging pipeline, from taxonomy-driven bug injection to LLM-based detection and repair, and finally to simulation-based validation, for framework-agnostic OpenQASM 3.0 programs. We further conduct a comprehensive case study using QBugLM to benchmark two LLMs, Claude 4.6 Sonnet and Qwen3 Coder Next, across different prompting strategies, bug categories, and quantum programs. Our results show that iterative feedback is critical, as a single retry raises Pass@1 from below 25% to above 80%. Moreover, simpler structured prompting can even outperform Chain-of-Thought and ReAct for reasoning-capable models under fixed-resource constraints. Our work takes initial steps toward benchmarking LLM capabilities for debugging quantum programs and offers practical insights to support future efforts in automated quantum software repair.

</details>


### [6] [AutoPipelineAI: Context-Aware CI/CD Pipeline Generation from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06662)
*Youssef Mohamed Aboelfotoh,Mohamed Ahmed Hemdan,Mohammad El-Ramly,Khlood Hassan,Mahmoud Saleh Saad,Ahmed Mohamed Tolba,Seif Gamal Abdelmonem*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了AutoPipelineAI系统，利用大语言模型根据自然语言描述自动生成CI/CD流水线配置（如GitHub Actions、GitLab CI/CD），集成仓库感知分析、自动验证与反馈机制，旨在降低DevOps配置复杂度，提升开发者生产力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件开发依赖CI/CD流水线实现自动化测试、构建和部署，但配置DevOps流水线需理解平台特定语法并手动创建配置文件，过程复杂耗时，易导致配置错误和生产力下降，尤其对DevOps经验有限的开发者而言挑战更大。

Method: AutoPipelineAI系统采用大语言模型，通过自然语言描述翻译开发者意图，结合仓库结构分析生成特定平台的流水线脚本（如GitHub Actions、GitLab CI/CD），并集成自动验证系统和反馈机制以确保生成管道的准确性和可用性。

Result: 论文提出了评估框架，从生成精度、配置有效性和设置工作量减少三个维度衡量系统性能。初步评估结果表明，基于仓库感知的自然语言驱动的CI/CD生成是一种可行且有前景的范式。

Conclusion: AutoPipelineAI展示了LLM能够简化DevOps配置的复杂性，增强开发者对持续交付方法的可及性，为降低DevOps配置门槛和实现更易用的软件交付自动化提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Modern software development relies on CI/CD pipelines to automate testing, building, and deployment operations. Configuring DevOps pipelines is challenging and time-consuming, as developers must understand platform-specific syntax and manually create configuration files. This complexity can lead to configuration errors and reduced productivity, especially for developers with limited DevOps experience. This paper introduces the AutoPipelineAI system, which generates CI/CD pipeline configurations using natural language descriptions. The proposed solution uses large language models (LLMs) to translate developer intent, analyze repository structures, and create specific pipeline scripts for environments like GitHub Actions and GitLab CI/CD. It integrates repository-aware analysis, automated validation systems, and a feedback mechanism that confirms the accuracy and usability of the created pipelines. We present the system architecture, its implementation, and an assessment framework designed to measure generation precision, configuration validity, and reduction in setup effort compared to manual pipeline creation. AutoPipelineAI illustrates how LLMs can simplify the complexity of DevOps configuration and enhance developer access to continuous delivery methods. Evaluation results provide early evidence that repository-aware, natural-language-driven CI/CD generation is a viable and promising paradigm for reducing the complexity of DevOps configuration and enabling more accessible software delivery automation.

</details>


### [7] [Tensor Algebraic Property Skeletons: Amplifying Property-Based Testing for AI Compilers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06747)
*Yuxin Qiu,Ben Limpanukorn,Seongmin Lee,Jiyuan Wang,Qian Zhang,Miryung Kim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Propilot是一个基于LLM的代理式属性测试框架，通过将张量代数知识表示为可复用的属性骨架，为DL编译器自动生成可执行的属性测试（PBT），有效检测语义错误和数值差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DL编译器测试方法（如模糊测试）能生成格式正确的输入，但无法捕获图变换和优化过程中应保持的代数不变量的语义漂移。虽然张量代数已被研究数十年，但尚未转化为DL编译器的可执行属性测试，因为需要联合构造算子、输入和测试预言。

Method: Propilot框架包含三个阶段：(1) 将张量代数知识表示为可复用的属性骨架，包含算子约束、形状/值规则和预言模板；(2) 针对目标编译器实例化骨架为可执行PBT，生成配对的张量计算图、具体张量输入和期望语义关系；(3) 在测试执行前验证每个PBT候选的有效性和安全性，利用验证反馈、执行结果和覆盖信号指导后续生成。

Result: 在TVM上使用212个算子和20个属性骨架进行评估，生成了4,579个PBT。与直接基于LLM的PBT生成相比，冗余度降低了49%，并消除了无效测试。该方法能有效发现语义错误和数值差异。

Conclusion: Propilot通过将张量代数知识结构化编码为属性骨架，解决了DL编译器属性测试中算子、输入和预言联合构造的挑战，显著提高了测试的质量和效率。

Abstract: Deep learning (DL) compilers such as TVM and ONNX-MLIR lower tensor computation graphs into optimized executables for target backends. Testing these AI compilers has made substantial progress in generating well-formed inputs in the context of fuzzing; however, such generation alone does not catch semantic drifts from algebraic invariants that graph transformations and optimizations are expected to preserve. While tensor algebra has been studied for decades, it has not been transformed into executable property-based tests (PBTs) for DL compilers because doing so requires jointly constructing operators, inputs, and test oracles. The central challenge is no longer generating well-formed inputs for fuzzing DL compilers, but bootstrapping executable PBTs with such inputs and oracles based on tensor algebra.
  We realize this vision in Propilot, an LLM-driven agentic property-based testing framework for DL compilers with GPT 5.5. First, Propilot represents tensor algebra knowledge as reusable property skeletons, each coupled with operator constraints, shape and value rules, and oracle templates. Second, given a target compiler, Propilot instantiates these skeletons into executable PBTs by generating paired tensor computation graphs, concrete tensor inputs, and expected semantic relations as oracles. Next, to prevent generated tests from degenerating into invalid or uninformative PBTs, Propilot validates each PBT candidate before execution for applicability and safety. Validation feedback, execution results, and coverage signals guide subsequent generation.
  We evaluate Propilot on TVM with 212 operators and 20 property skeletons, generating 4,579 PBTs. Compared with direct LLM-based PBT generation, Propilot reduces redundancy by 49% and eliminates invalid tests through explicit property skeletons. This effectiveness translates into finding semantic errors and numerical discrepancies.

</details>


### [8] [Pomona: Continuous Code Quality Improvement via Small, Automated Changes at Bloomberg](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06752)
*David Williams,Angelos Evripiotis,Serkan Kirbas,Harry Morgan,Sergey Magidovich,Peter Wainwright,Federica Sarro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Pomona，一个轻量级智能体工具，通过Kaizen理念实现自动化代码质量持续改进。它使用Scanning技能识别改进任务并优先排序，Repair技能生成约10行差异的小型PR。经过一个月的团队部署和10位资深工程师问卷评估，15/17个PR被成功合并，中位关闭时间不到2小时，8/10的工程师愿意采用Pomona。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码质量改进工具缺乏自动化、持续性的增量修复能力，且难以在保持工程师信任和效率的同时减少技术债务。作者受Kaizen（持续改进）哲学启发，旨在构建一个轻量级、人机协同的智能体工具，实现频繁低风险的代码质量改进。

Method: Pomona采用双技能架构：Scanning技能扫描代码库发现lint违规、技术债务标记和测试缺口等改进任务，并将其优先排序为结构化待办事项列表；Repair技能针对每个任务生成仅约10行差异的小型PR。采用人在回路中的设计，让工程师审查和批准每个PR，以确保信任和生产力。

Result: 在一个月的团队部署中，生成的17个PR中有15个被成功合并（88%合并率），中位关闭时间不到2小时。对10位资深工程师的问卷调查显示，8/10表示愿意采用Pomona，并对小差异尺寸和专注于代码质量改进表示赞赏。

Conclusion: Pomona的初步结果表明，轻量级、增量式、人在回路中的智能体工具在工业环境中有效可行。作者为研究人员和从业者提供了关于在工业中有效部署智能体的可操作见解，强调了小变更、低风险和持续改进策略的重要性。

Abstract: In this short experience paper, we present Pomona, a lightweight agentic tool that utilises agent skills for continuous automated code quality improvement. Inspired by the philosophy of Kaizen(TM), Pomona automates a cycle of discovery and incremental repair: a Scanning skill identifies improvement tasks (e.g., linting violations, technical debt markers, and test gaps) and prioritises them in a structured backlog, while a Repair skill generates tiny pull requests (PRs) targeting ~10 lines of diff. This human-in-the-loop design enables frequent, low-risk improvements while maintaining engineer trust and productivity and reducing technical debt. We evaluated Pomona through a one-month deployment in a team and a questionnaire distributed to 10 senior engineers. Our preliminary results are promising: 15 of 17 generated PRs were successfully merged with a median time-to-close of under 2 hours. Furthermore, 8/10 of surveyed engineers expressed a desire to adopt Pomona, praising small diff sizes and Pomona's focus on improving code quality. We conclude by discussing actionable insights for researchers and practitioners on strategies for effective agentic deployment in industry.

</details>


### [9] [Chiseling Out Efficiency: Structured Skeleton Supervision for Efficient Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06821)
*Yu Yu,Zhihong Sun,Jia Li,Yao Wan,Chuanyi Li,Hongyu Zhang,Ruyun Wang,Tao Huang,Zhi Jin,Ge Li,Chen Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EffiSkel 提出效率骨架（efficiency skeletons）框架，通过显式提取和学习高效代码中的抽象结构模式，在多任务学习下联合优化代码生成与骨架预测，显著提升 LLM 生成代码的功能正确性和运行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅通过模仿完整优化后的解决方案或迭代后处理来提升 LLM 生成代码的效率，未能显式编码实现高运行时性能所必需的结构化代码模式。本文旨在解决两大核心挑战：(1) 从复杂语法和控制流中提取并表征隐式的效率导向结构模式；(2) 在不破坏 LLM 语义训练稳定性的前提下有效学习这些模式。

Method: 提出 EffiSkel 框架：(1) 通过三种互补策略显式提取效率骨架（efficiency skeletons）——高效代码背后抽象、可复用的结构模式；(2) 将骨架集成到多任务学习范式中，联合优化代码生成任务和骨架预测任务。

Result: 在多个编程语言和基准上，EffiSkel 显著提升了功能正确性和效率。在 Mercury 上，基于 DeepSeek-Coder (7B)，Efficiency Ratio 相比 EffiCoder 提升 +11.11%，相比 CodeDPO 提升 +3.71%；Average Speedup 相比 EffiCoder 提升 +0.36，相比 CodeDPO 提升 +0.22。

Conclusion: 显式建模效率骨架能有效提升 LLM 生成代码的运行时性能，验证了结构化效率模式在代码生成中的关键作用。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are capable of generating syntactically correct and functionally complete programs, greatly streamlining software development. However, recent studies reveal that these programs typically execute substantially slower than human-optimized counterparts. Existing approaches to bridging this efficiency gap typically involve either iteratively optimizing code after generation or fine-tuning models on corpora of efficient code. Yet, these methods expose the model to efficiency signals only by mimicking complete, optimized solutions, without explicitly encoding the structural code patterns essential for achieving high runtime performance. Addressing this gap presents two core challenges: (1) extracting and representing latent, efficiency-oriented structural patterns embedded within complex syntax and control flows, and (2) effectively learning these patterns without destabilizing the semantic training of LLMs. To tackle these challenges, we propose EffiSkel, an efficiency skeleton-guided framework that explicitly extracts and learns efficiency skeletons-abstract, reusable structural patterns underpinning efficient code-by leveraging three complementary strategies. These skeletons are integrated into a multi-task learning regime that jointly optimizes code generation and skeleton prediction. Experiments across multiple programming languages and benchmarks demonstrate that EffiSkel significantly enhances both functional correctness and efficiency, resulting on Mercury with DeepSeek-Coder (7B) a +11.11% (vs. EffiCoder) and +3.71% (vs. CodeDPO) higher Efficiency Ratio (ER), and a +0.36 (vs. EffiCoder) and +0.22 (vs. CodeDPO) increase in Average Speedup (AS). These results highlight the effectiveness of explicitly modeling efficiency skeletons in improving the runtime performance of code generated by LLMs.

</details>


### [10] [SkelDPO: A Skeleton-Guided Direct Preference Optimization Framework for Efficient Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06826)
*Yu Yu,Chen Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SkelDPO框架，通过骨架引导的偏好优化，在代码生成中显式建模影响执行效率的结构因素，相比现有方法在Pass@1、Beyond@1和Effi@1上分别提升3-6%、3-7%和2-5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Code LLMs在追求语义正确性时，将完整程序作为单一优化目标，未能显式建模影响执行效率的结构因素，导致模型无法在细粒度层面学习高效实现所需的核心骨架特征。

Method: 提出SkelDPO框架：1) 从代码数据集中识别高效与低效实现，通过对比分析定位效率关键点，形成效率与非效率骨架的对齐信号；2) 在训练中引入联合代码与骨架偏好损失，使模型同时学习语义正确性和效率关键组件。

Result: 与仅依赖高效/低效代码偏好优化的SOTA方法相比，Pass@1提升3-6%，Beyond@1提升3-7%，Effi@1提升2-5%，在复杂任务上提升更显著。

Conclusion: SkelDPO提供了骨架级效率对齐的新视角，突破了传统偏好优化仅依赖正确性或效率对的局限。

Abstract: With the remarkable progress of Code Large Language Models (Code LLMs) in achieving semantic correctness, execution efficiency has become an increasingly important dimension for evaluating their practical utility. However, existing approaches typically treat full programs as a single optimization target during training, without explicitly modeling the structural factors that influence efficiency. As a result, although these models can generate semantically correct code, they fail to learn, at a fine-grained level, the underlying skeleton features that lead to efficient implementations. To address this limitation, we propose SkelDPO (Skeleton-Guided Direct Preference Optimization), a skeleton-guided preference optimization framework that systematically enhances the efficiency of code generation. SkelDPO first identifies efficient and inefficient implementations from the code dataset and, through comparative analysis, locates their efficiency-prone and inefficiency-prone points, forming alignment signals between efficiency and inefficiency skeletons. During training, a joint code and skeleton preference loss is introduced, enabling the model to learn semantic correctness while reinforcing its understanding of efficiency-critical components in code. Results show that SkelDPO consistently surpasses existing methods: compared with SOTA method that relies solely on efficient and inefficient code preference optimization, it improves Pass@1, Beyond@1, and Effi@1 by 3-6%, 3-7%, and 2-5%, with greater improvements observed on complex tasks. Overall, SkelDPO provides a new perspective on skeleton-level efficiency alignment, breaking the limitation of conventional preference optimization that relies solely on correctness or efficiency pairs. All datasets and source code are publicly available at: https://github.com/icpcSkelDPO/SkelDPO.

</details>


### [11] [LLM Agent-Assisted Reverse Engineering with Quantitative Readability Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06838)
*Neil Archibald,Ruben Thijssen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于量化可读性评分（QRS）的LLM代理方法，用于改进反编译C代码的可读性。通过三个阶段的研究演进，最终构建了包含结构相似性门控和三个独立可读性子指标的复合指标，使LLM代理能在保证正确性的前提下定向提升代码可读性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动反编译器生成的C代码功能正确但可读性差，现有LLM代理方法缺乏量化指导，导致覆盖率不足、改进不一致，且代理会以意外方式优化指标（产生结构等价但可读性更差的代码）。

Method: 三阶段研究演进：Phase 1基于Ghidra MCP的工具驱动转向（缺乏量化指导）；Phase 2仅用结构相似性验证（代理以意外方式优化指标）；最终提出QRS框架——复合指标，包含结构相似性门控 + 三个独立子指标（词汇惊讶度、结构简洁性、惯用质量）。

Result: QRS引导的精炼方法使LLM代理能够在不牺牲正确性的前提下，进行有针对性、一致可验证的可读性改进，解决了前两阶段覆盖率不足和指标优化偏离的问题。

Conclusion: 量化可读性评分（QRS）框架能有效指导LLM代理提升反编译代码可读性，并可作为更广泛逆向工程工作流（二进制提升、反编译清理、功能等价验证）中的一部分，但该工作流的具体实现不在本文范围内。

Abstract: Automatic decompilers produce functionally correct but often unreadable C code. This paper addresses one stage of the reverse engineering workflow: improving the readability of decompiled code using LLM agents guided by quantitative metrics.
  We present a three-phase research evolution. Phase 1 (tool-driven steering via Ghidra MCP) suffered from incomplete coverage and inconsistent improvements due to lack of quantitative guidance. Phase 2 (structural similarity validation alone) revealed that agents optimize for metrics in unintended ways, producing structurally equivalent but less readable code. Our contribution is the Quantitative Readability Score (QRS) framework, a composite metric combining a structural similarity gate with three independent readability sub-metrics (Lexical Surprisal, Structural Simplicity, and Idiomatic Quality).
  We demonstrate that QRS-guided refinement enables LLM agents to make targeted readability improvements without sacrificing correctness. We provide a discussion of the broader reverse engineering workflow (binary lifting, decompilation cleanup, and achieving functional equivalence) as context, however, it remains out of scope.

</details>


### [12] [Empirical Study on the Characteristics and Evolution of AI-usage in GitHub Repositories: Evidence from Code Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06843)
*Abdullah Al Mujahid,Preetha Chatterjee,Mia Mohammad Imran*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对35,361个GitHub评论和12,996条后续提交消息的分析，发现开发者使用AI工具主要用于代码实现、增强、调试、文档和测试；后续提交频繁涉及重构、功能集成和错误修复；随时间推移，AI引用从直接代码生成转向知识支持和代码增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多在孤立环境中评估LLM输出，缺乏对开发者在真实项目中如何调整和适应AI辅助代码的实证分析。

Method: 收集35,361条明确引用AI的GitHub评论及相关代码块；对500条评论进行开放编码以构建分类体系；使用两个基于LLM的分类器进行全数据标注，并通过Dawid-Skene期望最大化聚合预测；分析12,996条后续提交消息以研究代码演变；分析2022年12月至2026年3月的时间趋势。

Result: 开发者主要使用LLM进行代码实现（首要）、代码增强、调试、文档和测试。后续提交中频繁出现重构与清理、功能集成与扩展、错误修复。时间趋势显示AI引用从直接代码生成转向知识支持与代码增强。

Conclusion: AI工具不仅作为代码生成辅助工具，更逐渐成为协作支持机制，其输出需要开发者进行持续的优化、扩展和修正。

Abstract: Developers increasingly use AI tools such as ChatGPT, Copilot, and Claude in everyday software workflows, but prior studies often evaluate LLM outputs in isolation rather than examining how developers adapt them in real projects. We analyze 35,361 GitHub code comments that explicitly reference AI use and their associated code blocks. We first open-code 500 unique comments and code blocks to derive a taxonomy of AI-assisted development activities, then annotate the full dataset using two LLM-based classifiers and aggregate predictions with Dawid-Skene expectation-maximization. We also analyze 12,996 subsequent commit messages to study how AI-assisted code evolves after introduction, and examine temporal trends from December 2022 to March 2026. Our results show that developers primarily use LLMs for code implementation, followed by code enhancement, debugging, documentation, and testing. Subsequent commits frequently involve refactoring and cleanup, feature integration and extension, and bug fixing, indicating sustained human oversight in adapting AI-assisted code. Over time, AI-referencing comments shift from direct code generation toward knowledge and conceptual support and code enhancement. These findings suggest that AI tools are becoming embedded not only as code-generation aids, but also as collaborative support mechanisms whose outputs are refined, extended, and corrected by developers over time.

</details>


### [13] [From Custom Logic to APIs: Understanding and Recommending API Replacement Refactorings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06912)
*Bridget Nyirongo,Yanjie Jiang,Yuxia Zhang,Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对API替换重构进行实证研究，提出混合框架AKIRA，结合模式确定性启发式与重构感知知识库，在API替换推荐上达到90%召回率和88%精确率，并在外部数据集上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: API替换重构（用API调用替代自定义逻辑）未被充分探索，现有工具依赖预定义模板，难以捕获复杂的多语句语义等价模式，缺乏对此类重构机会的系统性理解与自动化支持。

Method: 对6个Java项目的166,299次提交进行挖掘，人工分析1,800次提交并验证366个实例；提出AKIRA混合框架，融合模式确定性启发式与重构感知知识库。

Result: 在人工标注数据集上召回率90%、精确率88%；在外部RETIWA数据集上，召回率从21%提升至81%，精确率从40%提升至78%。

Conclusion: 结合静态模式匹配与语义推理可有效支持复杂API替换重构的自动化推荐，验证了混合方法的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Software refactoring is essential for maintaining code quality. However, API replacement refactoring, which replaces custom logic with API calls, remains underexplored. Existing refactoring tools provide limited support for detecting such opportunities because they rely on predefined templates and have difficulty capturing complex, multi-statement semantic equivalents. To address this limitation, we conduct the first empirical study of API replacement refactorings by mining 166,299 commits across six open-source Java projects and manually analyzing a curated subset of 1,800 commits, from which we identify 366 validated instances to characterize their scope, categories, and recurring patterns. Based on these insights, we propose AKIRA (Adaptive Knowledge Discovery and Retrieval), a hybrid framework that integrates pattern-deterministic heuristics with a refactoring-aware knowledge base to assess the practical feasibility of recommending API replacement refactorings. Our evaluation shows that AKIRA achieves 90% recall and 88% precision on a manually curated dataset. Furthermore, on the external RETIWA dataset, AKIRA significantly improves the state of the art by increasing recall from 21% to 81% and precision from 40% to 78%. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of combining static pattern matching with semantic reasoning to support the automation of recommending complex API replacement refactorings.

</details>


### [14] [Porting Declarative UI to HarmonyOS: A Heuristic-guided LLM Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07085)
*Kunwu Zheng,Pengyu Xue,Zhen Yang,Xiran Lyu,Peishi Lai,Mengying Zhao,Yutian Tang,Huizhi Zhang,Xianhang Li,Linhao Wu,Chengyi Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ArkTrans是一种从Android (Kotlin Jetpack Compose) 和 iOS (SwiftUI) 到鸿蒙ArkUI的自动UI翻译方法，通过启发式骨架构建和语法修复规则，解决了编程语言不熟悉和严重语法混乱两大挑战，在100样本基准测试中实现了高达90.67%的可编译率和高视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鸿蒙系统作为新兴操作系统，需要大量从Android和iOS迁移软件的生态建设需求；但最新声明式UI范式（KJC/SwiftUI/ArkUI）使传统UI翻译方法失效；现有方法要么针对后端逻辑，要么针对传统命令式UI，无法适用于声明式UI的跨平台翻译。

Method: 提出ArkTrans两阶段翻译方法：(1) 启发式骨架构建——通过元数据提取从源代码中构建ArkUI基本结构，引导LLM进行初始翻译；(2) 语法后修复——基于经验性发现的模式匹配规则，修复LLM翻译后残留的语法错误。

Result: 构建了100样本的KJC/SwiftUI到ArkUI文件级并行翻译基准。直接/一次性提示的LLM无法翻译出任何可编译的UI页面，而ArkTrans翻译的文件最多有90.67%可成功编译，且具有高视觉保真度。

Conclusion: ArkTrans有效解决了声明式UI跨平台翻译中编程语言不熟悉和语法混乱两大挑战，显著优于直接使用LLM的翻译效果，为鸿蒙生态的软件迁移提供了可行的UI自动化翻译方案。

Abstract: As an emerging operating system, HarmonyOS has a significant demand for software migration from platforms such as Android and iOS, where the User Interface (UI) translation accounts for a critical link. However, the latest UI development has shifted to declarative paradigms, e.g., Kotlin Jetpack Compose (KJC) for Android, SwiftUI for iOS, and ArkUI for HarmonyOS, rendering prior translation approaches inapplicable, as they target either backend logic or legacy imperative UIs.
  As such, this paper targets ArkUI and proposes an automatic translation approach, namely ArkTrans, to port UI files from Android and iOS to HarmonyOS.
  ArkTrans overcomes two salient challenges during the translation: (1) Programming Language (PL) unfamiliarity, and (2) severe syntactic chaos. Towards the first challenge, ArkTrans heuristically constructs ArkUI skeletons by extracting metadata from source PL, thereby guiding LLMs' initial translation. As for the second challenge, ArkTrans executes empirically revealed post-fixing rules via pattern matching to repair most of the remaining syntactic errors. To examine the effectiveness of ArkTrans, we construct a 100-sample parallel UI page translation benchmark from KJC/SwiftUI to ArkUI at the file level. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LLMs with direct/one-shot prompting cannot translate a single compilable UI page. In contrast, at most 90.67\% ArkTrans-translated files can be successfully compiled with high visual fidelity.

</details>


### [15] [MetaConfigurator: AI-Assisted RDF Authoring from JSON Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07094)
*Felix Neubauer,Mahdi Jafarkhani,Kenichi Endo,Jürgen Pleiss,Benjamin Uekermann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个RDF创作视图，扩展了开源JSON Schema编辑器MetaConfigurator，使研究人员能够在统一的Web界面中，通过AI辅助的RML映射将JSON/YAML/CSV数据转换为RDF，并进行三元组精炼、SPARQL查询、知识图谱可视化和RDF序列化导出。以MOF合成实验数据为例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工作流生成的结构化JSON数据缺乏语义互操作性，JSON Schema仅提供结构验证但不支持链接数据语义，现有工具难以在统一环境中完成从结构化数据到语义化RDF数据的转换与管理。

Method: 扩展MetaConfigurator编辑器，集成AI辅助RML映射、本体感知IRI自动补全、JSON-LD文本视图与RDF三元组表的双向同步、以及自然语言提示驱动的AI辅助SPARQL查询生成等功能，构建一体化Web界面工作流。

Result: 在MOF合成实验数据上验证了工作流：协议数据（试剂、步骤、数量）通过RML映射从JSON转换为基于本体的JSON-LD，实现了语义表示精炼、实验条件与结果关系查询及知识图谱交互式探索。

Conclusion: 该集成环境桥接了传统结构化数据管理与语义网技术，在保留实验上下文的同时通过AI辅助降低了技术门槛。

Abstract: Scientific workflows increasingly generate structured JSON data that is easy to exchange but difficult to interpret consistently across systems due to lacking semantic interoperability. While JSON Schema ensures structural validation, it provides no native support for Linked Data semantics.
  This paper presents an RDF Authoring View extending the open-source JSON Schema editor MetaConfigurator, enabling researchers to transform existing JSON, YAML, or CSV data into RDF using AI-assisted RML mappings, refine triples, execute SPARQL queries, visualize knowledge graphs, and export RDF serializations within a single integrated web interface. This workflow is supported by ontology-aware IRI auto-completion, bidirectional synchronization between JSON-LD text views and RDF triple tables, and AI-assisted SPARQL query generation from natural language hints.
  We demonstrate the workflow using laboratory data from metal-organic framework (MOF) synthesis experiments. Protocol data describing reagents, procedure steps, and quantities is converted from JSON to ontology-based JSON-LD via RML mappings. We then refine the semantic representation, query relationships between experimental conditions and outcomes, and explore the resulting knowledge graph interactively. This integrated environment bridges conventional structured data management with Semantic Web technologies while preserving experimental context and lowering technical barriers through AI assistance.

</details>


### [16] [SWE-Explore: Benchmarking How Coding Agents Explore Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07297)
*Shaoqiu Zhang,Yuhang Wang,Jialiang Liang,Yuling Shi,Wenhao Zeng,Maoquan Wang,Shilin He,Ningyuan Xu,Siyu Ye,Kai Cai,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SWE-Explore基准，用于隔离评估代码代理的仓库探索能力。该基准覆盖848个issue、10种编程语言、203个开源仓库，通过独立代理轨迹蒸馏行级标注。实验表明，探索指标（覆盖度、排序、上下文效率）与下游修复行为强相关，代理型探索器显著优于经典检索方法，行级覆盖和高效排序是区分最先进探索器的关键维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级编码基准（如SWE-bench）将编码任务视为整体二分类预测（解决/未解决），忽略了细粒度能力（如仓库理解、上下文检索、代码定位、缺陷诊断）的评估，尤其缺乏对仓库探索这一关键能力的独立评测。

Method: 构建SWE-Explore基准，包含848个issue、10种编程语言、203个开源仓库。从成功解决同一issue的独立代理轨迹中蒸馏出行级ground truth（具体 consulted 的代码区域）。评估维度包括覆盖度、排序质量和上下文效率。

Result: 探索指标与下游修复行为强相关。代理型探索器整体显著优于经典检索方法。文件级定位已较强，行级覆盖和高效排序是区分最先进探索器的关键维度。

Conclusion: SWE-Explore能够有效隔离评估代码代理的仓库探索能力，行级覆盖和排序质量是未来提升方向。

Abstract: Repository-level coding benchmarks such as SWE-bench have driven a rapid surge in the capabilities of coding agents. Yet they usually treat coding tasks as a holistic, binary prediction problem (e.g., resolved or unresolved), neglecting fine-grained agent capabilities such as repository understanding, context retrieval, code localization, and bug diagnosis. In this paper, we introduce SWE-Explore, a benchmark that isolates the evaluation of repository exploration, a critical capability of coding agents. Given a repository and an issue, SWE-Explore asks an explorer to return a ranked list of relevant code regions under a fixed line budget. SWE-Explore covers 848 issues across 10 programming languages and 203 open-source repositories. For each instance, we derive line-level ground truth from independent agent trajectories that successfully solved the same issue, distilling the specific code regions their solution paths actually consulted. We evaluate exploration along coverage, ranking, and context-efficiency dimensions, showing that these metrics strongly track downstream repair behavior. Across a broad set of retrieval methods, general coding agents, and specialized localizers, we find that agentic explorers form a clear tier above classical retrieval. While file-level localization is already strong for modern methods, line-level coverage and efficient ranking remain the key axes differentiating state-of-the-art explorers.

</details>


### [17] [Is US Defense Acquisition Ready to Acquire AI-Enabled Capabilities? Assessing the DoD Software Acquisition Pathway Through a Scenario-Based Policy Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07393)
*Daniel Lugo,James C. Davis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估美国国防部软件采办路径（SWP）是否足以应对AI系统采办的独特需求。通过基于场景的评估发现，SWP治理体系为迭代交付和AI测试提供了可行基础，但存在核心指导的可操作性不足问题：AI特定的数据溯源、生命周期管理和人类监督控制分散在补充文件中，导致项目办公室依赖不一致的本地解读。建议创建AI支持性子路径并改进关键工件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统从实验原型转变为关键任务工具，其对动态数据、演化模型和治理的依赖引发疑问：现有采办路径能否跟上步伐。美国国防部通过适应性采办框架进行了现代化改革，但SWP是否足以应对AI采办的独特需求尚待评估。

Method: 采用基于场景的评估方法，追踪一个虚拟AI赋能项目通过SWP关键规划活动的过程，运用政策场景分析评估以SWP为中心的治理体系是否为AI采办提供了可操作的支持。

Result: SWP治理体系为迭代交付和AI测试提供了可行基础，但存在核心指导的可操作性不足问题。AI特定的数据溯源、生命周期管理和人类监督控制分散在补充文件中，而非嵌入执行SWP的程序面对机制，导致项目办公室依赖不一致的本地解读。

Conclusion: 建议创建AI支持性子路径并改进目标工件，以弥合政策到工件的差距。

Abstract: As AI systems transition from experimental prototypes to mission-critical tools, their dependence on dynamic data, evolving models, and governance raises questions about whether existing acquisition pathways can keep pace. The U.S. Department of Defense has modernized its acquisition processes through the Adaptive Acquisition Framework, with the Software Acquisition Pathway (SWP) serving as the primary mechanism for acquiring software-intensive capabilities. This paper evaluates whether SWP is sufficient to address the unique demands of AI acquisition.
  In this work, we perform a scenario-based evaluation that traces a notional AI-enabled program through key SWP planning activities to assess how policy translates into program artifacts and decisions. We use Policy Scenario Analysis to examine whether the SWP-centered governance stack provides sufficient actionable support for AI acquisition. The governance stack provides a viable foundation for iterative delivery and AI testing. However, we identify a recurring actionability problem in the core guidance. AI-specific controls for data provenance, lifecycle management, and human oversight remain distributed across supplemental documents rather than embedded in the program-facing mechanisms through which SWP is executed. This disconnect leaves program offices reliant on inconsistent local interpretation. We conclude by recommending an AI-supporting sub-path and targeted artifact refinements to better bridge this policy-to-artifact gap.

</details>


### [18] [Socratic-SWE: Self-Evolving Coding Agents via Trace-Derived Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07412)
*Chuan Xiao,Zhengbo Jiao,Shaobo Wang,Wei Wang,Bing Zhao,Hu Wei,Linfeng Zhang,Lin Qu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Socratic-SWE框架，通过闭环自我进化机制，将历史解题轨迹蒸馏为结构化技能，用于生成针对性的修复任务，从而持续提升LLM驱动的软件工程智能体性能，在多个基准上达到最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的软件工程智能体训练受限于高质量SWE任务数据的稀缺性，而现有的合成数据方法通过固定变异或缺陷注入程序生成任务，生成的分布与智能体自身的弱点和训练进度无关，缺乏自适应性和针对性。

Method: Socratic-SWE是一个闭环自我进化框架。1) 将智能体历史解题轨迹蒸馏为结构化技能（总结重复失败模式和有效修复模式）；2) 利用这些技能指导在真实仓库中生成针对性修复任务；3) 通过基于执行的验证检查候选任务；4) 使用求解器梯度对齐奖励评分，确保保留的任务既可验证又有助于改进求解器；5) 更新后的求解器产生新轨迹，使课程任务能够多轮自适应。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified（50.40%）、SWE-bench Lite、SWE-bench Pro和Terminal-Bench 2.0上，Socratic-SWE在相同计算预算下持续优于自我进化基线方法。

Conclusion: 解题轨迹可以作为自我进化SWE智能体的可扩展基板，Socratic-SWE框架通过闭环自我进化有效提升了智能体的实际软件工程能力。

Abstract: LLM-driven software engineering agents have become a central testbed for real-world language-model capability, yet their training remains limited by the availability of high-quality SWE tasks. Existing synthetic data methods typically create tasks through fixed mutation or bug-injection procedures, making the resulting distributions largely independent of the agent's own weaknesses and training progress. We introduce Socratic-SWE, a closed-loop self-evolution framework that reuses the agent's historical solving traces as a source of training signal. Rather than treating traces only as evidence for reward computation, Socratic-SWE distills them into structured agent skills that summarize recurring failures and effective repair patterns. These skills then guide the generation of targeted repair tasks in real repositories. Candidate tasks are checked through execution-based validation and scored with a solver-gradient alignment reward, so that the retained tasks are both verifiable and useful for improving the Solver. The updated Solver produces new traces, enabling the task curriculum to adapt over successive rounds. Across SWE-bench Verified, SWE-bench Lite, SWE-bench Pro, and Terminal-Bench 2.0, Socratic-SWE consistently improves over self-evolving baselines under the same compute budget, reaching 50.40% on SWE-bench Verified after three iterations. These results suggest that solving traces can serve as a scalable substrate for self-evolving SWE agents.

</details>


### [19] [Agentic Very Much! Adoption of Coding Agent in New GitHub Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07448)
*Romain Robbes,Théo Matricon,Thomas Degueule,Andre Hora,Stefano Zacchiroli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究分析了新创建的GitHub项目中编码助手的采用情况，发现采用率是之前研究的两倍以上，且AI辅助提交的比例显著更高，尽管存在检测不完全的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续先前关于GitHub项目中编码助手采用情况的研究，进一步探究新创建项目中的采用趋势和强度变化。

Method: 对先前研究之后创建的新GitHub项目样本进行分析，比较编码助手的采用率和AI辅助提交的比例。

Result: 新样本中编码助手的采用率是先前研究的两倍以上，且AI辅助提交的比例显著更高，尽管检测手段可能未能完全捕捉所有AI辅助活动。

Conclusion: 编码助手在GitHub项目中的采用呈现快速增长趋势，采用强度和广度均在增加，表明AI辅助编码正在成为软件开发的主流实践。

Abstract: In previous work, we investigated the adoption of coding agents in GitHub projects, finding that it was very significant. This study follows this line of work, but analyses new projects, that were created after the previous study. In this new sample, we find that the adoption of coding agents is more than twice as high. We also find that the adoption is significantly more intensive, as the proportion of AI-assisted commits is sensibly higher, despite strong signs that we do not detect all of it.

</details>

### [20] [Depth over Fidelity in Fixed-Budget Noisy Evolution Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06555)
*Sichen Wang,Zhipeng Lu*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 针对固定评估预算下噪声进化策略中深度-保真度权衡问题，提出概率精英成员（PEM）方法，用条件期望排名权重替代硬排名权重，实现排名不确定性的Rao-Blackwell化降噪，并通过残差自举（RB-PEM）和自适应探针切换机制在多种任务上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定评估预算下，噪声进化策略面临深度-保真度权衡：花费评估去噪代内排名会减少优化器可执行的分布更新次数。现有方法在这两者间难以取得良好平衡。

Method: 提出概率精英成员（PEM），用条件期望排名权重替代硬排名权重，通过对排名不确定性积分实现Rao-Blackwell化降噪；通过残差自举（RB-PEM）实例化，并辅以自适应探针-切换机制应对低噪声场景。

Result: 在COCO bbob-noisy套件、RL策略搜索和超参数优化任务中，RB-PEM在高噪声、预算受限场景下持续取得性能提升。

Conclusion: 在固定评估预算下应优先深度（更多分布更新）而非保真度（更精确排名），PEM方法通过概率化排名权重有效缓解了噪声影响。

Abstract: Noisy evolution strategies under fixed evaluation budgets face a depth-fidelity trade-off: spending evaluations to denoise intra-generation rankings reduces the number of distribution updates the optimizer can execute. We argue for depth over fidelity and propose probabilistic elite membership (PEM), which replaces hard rank-based weights in evolution strategies with conditional expected rank weights that integrate over ranking uncertainty. PEM preserves the conditional mean update while reducing conditional update dispersion, a Rao-Blackwellization of the noisy rank-based step. We instantiate PEM via residual bootstrapping (RB-PEM) with capped per-generation overhead, complemented by an adaptive probe-and-switch mechanism for low-noise regimes. Across the COCO bbob-noisy suite and external tasks including RL policy search and hyperparameter optimization, RB-PEM achieves consistent gains in high-misranking, budget-constrained settings.

</details>


### [21] [A Data-Free Symbolic Regression Approach for Solving Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07152)
*Sergei Garmaev,Vinay Sharma,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出符号方程求解器（SES），将方程求解转化为可微符号模型的优化问题，无需配对数据即可从方程本身恢复显式符号解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归方法需要从底层过程获取训练数据，而非仅依赖控制方程本身，限制了其在仅有方程时应用。

Method: 将方程求解建模为可微符号模型上的优化问题，目标函数由方程及初始/边界条件构建，学习得到显式符号表达式。

Result: 在代数方程、超越方程、常微分方程和偏微分方程等各类方程上，SES恢复了与解析解匹配的紧凑符号表达式。

Conclusion: SES无需配对输入-输出数据即可从方程中学习显式符号解，为科学计算中无法通过传统方法求解的方程提供新途径。

Abstract: Many equations arising in science currently cannot be solved by available analytical techniques and are therefore solved numerically, without yielding explicit symbolic expressions. Existing symbolic regression approaches can recover symbolic expressions, but require training data obtained from the underlying process, rather than the governing equation alone. We propose the Symbolic Equation Solver (SES), a framework that formulates equation solving as an optimization problem over differentiable symbolic models. SES constructs its objective from the equation together with initial or boundary conditions, eliminating the need for paired input-output data. The learned model is expressed in explicit symbolic form, enabling further analysis. We evaluate SES on representative algebraic and differential equations, including a system of algebraic equations, an equation with transcendental terms, an ordinary differential equation, and partial differential equations with different initial or boundary conditions. Across these settings, SES recovers compact symbolic expressions that match the corresponding analytical solutions.

</details>


### [22] [Combinatorial Landscape Analysis for Dominating Set and Vertex Coloring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07361)
*Johanna Gasse,Antonia Heinen,Felix Knöfel,Timo Kötzing,Maxim Stanko*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文分析了支配集和顶点着色两个组合优化问题在不同图类上的局部最优结构，考察了两种邻域算子（单点变动和交换变动）下的景观模态：单峰、高原单峰、等模态或真正多模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解组合优化问题中局部最优解的结构特性，有助于设计更有效的启发式算法和局域搜索策略。

Method: 针对支配集和顶点着色问题，在多种图类上分别采用两种邻域算子（单点变动和交换变动），分析局部最优解的空间结构（单峰、高原单峰、等模态、多模态）。

Result: 确定了不同图类下支配集和顶点着色问题在两种邻域算子下的景观模态类别。

Conclusion: 不同图类和邻域算子对局部最优结构有显著影响，部分问题在图类上呈现等模态性质。

Abstract: We analyze the two combinatorial problems of Dominating Set and Vertex Coloring regarding what kind of local optima are present for various instances. For a variety of graph classes each, we determine whether the induced landscapes are unimodal, plateau-unimodal (all optima are just one plateau), equimodal (all local optima are global) or truly multimodal. We do this for two different neighborhood operators, one based on making only a single change and one also allowing swaps (interchanging two parts of the solution).

</details>

### [23] [Belief-Aware Scheduling for Predictive Wildfire Hazard Mapping under Sparse-Window Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06917)
*Xun Shao,Kohsuke Yamakawa,Cheah Wai Shiang*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文研究在监测野火的边缘节点下行链路受限条件下，如何通过结构化信念（structured belief）和调度器来预测H步后的火灾风险图，将问题形式化为部分可观测的序列分配问题，并证明在正确构建信念与调度问题时，轻量级架构即可取得优于深度模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘节点监测野火时，下行链路存在 duty-limited 或窗口容量限制，接收端需从有限传输数据中预测未来H步的火灾风险图。核心问题不是选择何种神经网络架构，而是如何推导出足以支持预测任务的结构化信念，并通过调度器维护该信念，以应对未来传输机会的不确定性。

Method: 将问题形式化为部分可观测的序列分配问题，包含三个耦合的每区域动作轴（感知、表示、传输）；从H步前向算子的输入需求推导结构化信念的每个组件；在物理校准的合成环境中独立控制窗口周期P、每窗口容量C、预测视界H和燃料组成等参数进行实验。

Result: 1）非短视活动节奏参考与均匀节奏之间的差距在窗口周期稀疏度上呈单峰分布，在中等间隔处达到峰值；2）消融结构化信念时，主导组件在默认景观（时间陈旧性）和结构化景观（静态风险先验）之间切换，而逐单元强度信念在两者中均为冗余；3）40k参数的轻量级跨区域注意力编码器在默认景观上超出FAIR活动节奏参考约28%，在结构化景观上超出约11%；更深层的Transformer编码器并未提升平均预测损失，且训练种子方差更高。

Conclusion: 在该任务类别和设定下，当信念和调度问题被正确形式化时，适度的架构归纳偏置足以取得良好性能，无需依赖深层模型。

Abstract: An edge node monitoring a wildfire observes more than a duty-limited or windowed down-link can carry. The receiver must predict the H-step-ahead hazard map from whatever the link delivers. We argue the operative design problem is not which neural architecture to use but how to derive a structured belief sufficient for the receiver's prediction task and maintain it through a scheduler that anticipates future transmission opportunities. We formalize this as a partially observed sequential allocation problem with three coupled per-region action axes (sensing, representation, transmission), and derive each component of the structured belief from the H-step forward operator's input requirements. Identifying these mechanisms requires independent control over the window period P, per-window capacity C, predictive horizon H, and fuel composition, which is not separable in real-landscape data; we therefore evaluate on a physics-calibrated synthetic environment. Three empirical observations support the principle: the gap between a non-myopic activity-paced reference and uniform pacing is unimodal in window-period sparsity, peaking at intermediate spacing; ablating the structured belief, the dominant operative component flips between a default landscape (temporal staleness) and a structured landscape (static-risk prior), while the per-cell intensity belief is redundant in both; and a 40 k-parameter lightweight cross-region attention encoder exceeds the FAIR activity-paced reference by ~28% on the default landscape and ~11% on the structured landscape. A deeper Transformer encoder does not improve over the lightweight encoder in mean predictive loss and exhibits higher training-seed variance. Within this task class and regime, a modest architectural inductive bias suffices when the belief and the scheduling problem are correctly posed.

</details>


### [24] [The Three-Ring Architecture: Governing Agents in the Era of On-Platform Organisations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07119)
*Sergio Alvarez-Telena,Marta Diez-Fernandez*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出三环架构（Three-Ring Architecture）作为企业AI治理的基础设施：环1为现有生产架构，环2为基于策略的确定性M2联邦层（相当于智能体的操作系统），环3为基于LLM的非确定性前沿智能层。核心贡献在于区分环2与环3的风险特征——环2可追溯、可执行、可恢复，环3则引入不可追溯的非确定性风险。环2不是可选增强，而是控制与合规的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前企业AI部署面临结构性失败：组织获取了智能体能力，却缺乏治理基础设施，预计将重蹈第一波AI部署中因缺乏联邦层导致95%项目失败率的覆辙。

Method: 形式化定义了三环架构：环1为现有生产架构；环2为基于策略的智能体M2联邦层，执行资源抽象、流程协调、权限执行和智能复合平台功能；环3为基于LLM的前沿智能层。从技术精确性上论证环2相当于企业级操作系统，并严格区分环2（确定性、可追溯、可恢复）与环3（非确定性、不可追溯）的风险谱系。

Result: 策略型智能体在确定性框架内运行——后果可追溯、权限可执行、偏差可恢复。LLM型智能体引入范畴上不同的风险：非确定性执行者，其偏差在复杂组织系统中传播而无法追溯。LLM能力提升是结构性的顺风——能力越强，偏差后果越大，治理需求随之增长。该架构已在金融、政府、采购、合规等领域经过十年部署验证。

Conclusion: 环2不是有用补充，而是控制与合规的必要条件。每一轮LLM能力提升都为该架构提供结构性推动力，因为更强能力的非确定性执行者在偏离时产生更大后果，治理需求与能力同步增长。

Abstract: The current phase of enterprise AI deployment faces a structural failure: organisations are acquiring agentic capability without the infrastructure to govern it. The result is expected to reproduce the error of the first wave of AI deployment: decentralised intelligence without a federation layer leading to a 95% project failure rate. This paper formalises the Three-Ring Architecture as the governing infrastructure of the on-platform organisation. Ring 1 is the existing production architecture; Ring 2 is the M2 federation layer built on strategies-based agentic AI; Ring 3 is the LLM-based frontier intelligence layer. Ring 2 constitutes, in the technically exact sense, the operating system of the agentic enterprise - performing at the organisational level what a computing OS performs at the device level: resource abstraction, process coordination, permission enforcement, and a stable platform for compounding intelligence. A central contribution is the formal distinction between Ring 2 and Ring 3 risk profiles. Strategies-based agents operate within a deterministic framework: their consequences are traceable, their permissions enforceable, their deviations recoverable. LLM-based agents introduce a categorically distinct risk: a non-deterministic actor whose deviations propagate through complex organisational systems without retrospective traceability. Ring 2 is not a useful addition - it is a necessary condition of control and compliance. A further consequence: every improvement in LLM capability is a structural tailwind for this architecture. More capable non-deterministic actors produce larger consequences when they deviate. The governance requirement scales with capability. The architecture has been validated across a decade of deployment in financial services, government, procurement, and compliance among other sectors.

</details>


### [25] [Distributed Persistence Domain for Persistent Memory Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07159)
*Khan Shaikhul Hadi,Andres David Delgado,Naveed Ul Mustafa,Mark Heinrich,Hao Zheng,Yan Solihin*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出分布式持久域(DPD)抽象，通过在CXL交换机中集成持久性支持来降低持久内存池化的延迟，并设计了持久性CXL交换机架构（平均加速33%，读转发优化下最高36%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CXL内存池化中集成持久化语义会引入显著延迟，因为持久操作必须穿越整个CXL架构（包括交换机、链路和协议层）才能到达远程持久内存。将持久支持移到网络内部会打破传统集中式持久域的正确性假设。

Method: 提出分布式持久域(DPD)抽象，形式化定义分布式持久域概念；基于正确性分析推导设计需求；设计持久性CXL交换机架构，支持持久性操作、读转发和写合并，同时保证崩溃一致性。

Result: 使用SPLASH-4和YCSB基准测试进行仿真评估，相比易失性CXL交换机平均加速33%，读转发优化在所有工作负载下实现最高36%加速。

Conclusion: 在CXL交换机层面支持持久性是提升持久内存池化可扩展性的有效方向，DPD抽象为分布式持久域的正确性保证提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Compute Express Link (CXL) enables memory pooling over disaggregated memory, offering the potential to improve resource utilization in persistent memory systems. However, integrating persistence semantics into CXL-based memory pooling introduces substantial latency, which limits system scalability. This overhead arises because persist operations must traverse the entire CXL fabric, including switches, links, and protocol layers, before reaching remote persistent memory. To this end, we argue that extending CXL switches with persistence support is a promising direction for improving the scalability of persistent memory pooling. However, moving persistence support into the network breaks the traditional correctness assumptions of centralized persistence domains. In particular, enabling persistence within distributed structures, such as CXL switches, can introduce stale reads and writes if not carefully coordinated.
  In this paper, we propose Distributed Persistence Domain (DPD), a new abstraction for persistent memory pooling that enables persistence support at the CXL switch level. We first formalize the concept of a distributed persistence domain and use DPD as a framework to identify the correctness hazards that arise when persistence structures are distributed across the CXL fabric. Based on this analysis, we derive the design requirements needed to guarantee correctness. Building on these insights, we present Persistent CXL Switch, a CXL switch architecture that incorporates persistence support to significantly reduce persist latency, enable read forwarding, and coalesce writes, while preserving correctness and crash consistency. We evaluated our system design using both SPLASH-4 and YCSB benchmarks. Simulation results show an average speedup of 33% over volatile CXL switches, and up to 36% speedup with read forwarding optimization across all workloads.

</details>

### [26] [LinkNav: Surfacing Interconnected Information in Scientific Articles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06650)
*Sebastian Joseph,Jennifer Healey,Junyi Jessy Li,Ani Nenkova*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: LinkNav通过语言模型生成阅读时可能提出的问题，并在论文其他位置搜索答案，建立非相邻段落间的显式连接，帮助读者捕捉可能遗漏的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术论文阅读中，读者常因内容分散在不同位置而难以发现段落间的关联，现有阅读体验缺乏对非相邻但相关段落间的显式连接支持。

Method: 使用语言模型为当前段落生成可能出现的疑问，然后在文档其他位置搜索答案段落，当找到答案时建立文档内部连接。

Result: 答案检测流水线具有高精度，能为文档生成合理数量的连接；在学术论文数据集上，被连接段落平均间隔10个段落的距离。

Conclusion: LinkNav能够有效建立跨段落的显式连接，帮助读者发现可能被忽略的文档内部关联。

Abstract: We present LinkNav, an enhanced experience for reading academic papers which makes explicit connections between related but non-adjacent passages. To create the experience, we instruct a language model to generate questions that may arise while reading a passage and then search for answer passages elsewhere in the document, forming intra-document connections when answers are found. We confirm that these building blocks work well to power the experience, with an answer detection pipeline that works with high precision, resulting in a reasonable number of connections being made for a document. On a dataset of academic papers, we find that connected passages are on average ten segments away from each other, making explicit connections that a reader may have otherwise missed.

</details>


### [27] [Adversarial Co-Thinking: Calibration and Triangulation Across Multiple GenAI Tools in HCI Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06702)
*Pia Tukkinen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了当GenAI工具被完全嵌入学术论文起草过程（而非仅限于后期润色）时会发生什么。作者通过自身写作实验，提出“对抗性共思”（adversarial co-thinking）这一概念：利用过去的同行评审来校准工具，然后让多个工具的输出相互对抗测试，而非盲目遵从。作者认为，从默认称赞的工具中引出真正的批评是核心挑战，而关键技能在于评估而非生成。该实践是高技能的认识论实践，能放大已有专长，但也可能掩盖其缺失。当前披露框架难以捕捉这一转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究GenAI工具从“后期辅助”转向“全程嵌入”学术写作时，会带来怎样的实践变化和认识论挑战，尤其是如何从默认顺从/赞美的AI工具中获取真正的批判性反馈。

Method: 作者以自身为实验对象，从论文第一句开始同时使用三种GenAI工具（Claude、ChatGPT、Gemini）进行起草，并将它们的输出与自己的预期贡献进行平行比较，形成系统化的对抗性共思流程。

Result: 提出“对抗性共思”概念：利用过往同行评审校准工具，让多工具输出相互对抗测试；发现从默认称赞的工具中引出真诚批评是核心实践挑战；揭示此类实践是高技能的认识论行为，能放大但也能掩盖专长；指出现有披露框架不足以应对这种转变。

Conclusion: 提出四项供研讨会讨论的命题：学术自主性、导师监督、使用机会的公平性、以及披露机制改革。

Abstract: This paper examines what happens when GenAI tools are fully embedded in the drafting of an academic paper rather than confined to late-stage polishing. To investigate how an intensive multi-tool GenAI workflow differs from conventional academic writing, I drafted this paper from the first sentence in parallel with three GenAI tools - Claude, ChatGPT, and Gemini - comparing their outputs against my own intended contribution. Across this process, a recurring pattern took shape that I call adversarial co-thinking: using past peer reviews to calibrate the tools, then setting their outputs against one another to be tested rather than deferred to. I argue that surfacing genuine critique from tools that default to praise is a central practical challenge of working with these tools, and that the skill at stake is evaluative rather than generative. Adversarial co-thinking is a high-skill epistemic practice: it can amplify expertise where it exists, but it can also mask its absence. I further argue that current disclosure frameworks are poorly equipped to capture this shift. The paper offers four propositions for workshop discussion concerning autonomy, supervision, equity of access, and disclosure.

</details>


### [28] [Exploring Reinforcement Learning for Fluid Transitions Between Clinical Mental Healthcare and Everyday Wellness Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06800)
*Tony Wang,Qian Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探索使用强化学习（RL）构建整合临床与健康干预的数字化系统，部署上下文赌博机动态选择日志提示以优化持续写作目标。通过4周探索性研究（N=38）发现：RL优化序列的益处多在干预结束后显现，且高参与用户呈现深度参与模式，而恒定干预组出现倦怠脱落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心理健康干预中临床与健康干预孤立运行，导致护理过渡频繁中断，亟需一种能主动整合两类干预并形成连贯护理路径的系统。

Method: 构建上下文赌博机（contextual bandit）模型，动态从临床和健康干预库中选择日志提示，以优化整体健康目标（持续日志写作），并开展4周探索性研究（N=38）。

Result: 1) RL优化的干预序列益处主要在干预结束后显现；2) RL生成干预的高参与用户随时间加深参与，而恒定干预组高参与用户易出现倦怠和脱落。

Conclusion: 提出了两个关键设计问题：1) 系统是否应包含"退步期"以及何时/如何设置；2) 系统何时应降低强度以防倦怠、何时应维持强度以最大化治疗效果。

Abstract: Mental health struggles wax and wane, yet clinical and wellness interventions typically operate separately, causing frequent breakdowns at care transitions. We explore reinforcement learning (RL) as a means to build digital health systems that deliver clinical and wellness interventions proactively, as part of a coherent care journey. We ask: what complexities does designing such a system involve? We built a contextual bandit that dynamically selects journaling prompts from clinical and wellness repertoires to optimize for an overarching health goal (sustained journaling) and deployed it in a four-week exploratory study (N=38). We found that, first, many benefits of RL-optimized intervention sequences appeared only after interventions ended, raising the question: Should systems that offer coherent clinical-wellness care journeys include stepping-back periods? If so, when and how? Second, participants most engaged with RL-generated interventions deepened their engagement over time, while those most engaged with a constant intervention tended to burn out and drop out later. It raises the question: When should a system blending clinical and wellness interventions reduce intensity to prevent burnout in versus sustain it to maximize treatment gains?

</details>


### [29] [Personality Anchoring for Social Simulation: Linking Personality, Social Behavior, and Interaction Success with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06936)
*Vahid Sadiri Javadi,Aksa Aksa,Fryderyk Róg,Lucie Flek,Johanne R. Trippas*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过引入基于CHARISMA框架的模拟管道，采用人格锚定方法利用知名电影角色和公众人物作为智能体，对1,010场模拟对话进行大规模实证研究，发现二元宜人性组合与共享目标达成之间存在单调关系，同质高宜人性配对成功率达62%，而同质低宜人性配对仅为6%。行为中介分析显示宜人性部分通过合作策略选择影响目标达成，但即使在相同主导策略内仍存在预测效应，表明存在可观察对话行为之外的路径。稳健性分析验证了结果的高度一致性和人格锚定的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究缺乏系统探究不同人格配置如何在社会情境中共同影响社交行为的方法论工具，尤其是二元层面的人格组合效应。

Method: 采用改编自CHARISMA框架的模拟管道，通过人格锚定方法将知名电影角色和公众人物作为心理学上有依据的智能体，进行多LLM社会模拟，并开展大规模实证研究分析二元宜人性组合对社交互动结果的影响。

Result: 二元宜人性组合与共享目标达成之间存在单调关系，同质高宜人性配对成功率为62%，同质低宜人性配对仅6%；行为中介分析表明宜人性部分通过合作策略选择影响目标达成；重复模拟结果高度一致（ICC=0.89），验证了人格锚定的有效性。

Conclusion: 人格锚定是一种可行的人格操作性策略，可用于系统性研究人格配置对社会互动的影响；宜人性通过合作策略及超越可观察行为的多条路径影响社交结果。

Abstract: Social interactions are shaped by the interplay of dispositional traits and situational context, yet systematically investigating how personality configurations between individuals jointly influence social behavior across diverse social contexts remains methodologically challenging. We address this gap by introducing a simulation pipeline adapted from the CHARISMA framework, which employs well-known movie characters and public figures as psychologically grounded agents for multi-LLM social simulation using a method we term personality anchoring. We present a large-scale empirical study examining how dyadic Agreeableness composition influences social interaction outcomes across 1,010 simulated conversations. Our results reveal a monotonic relationship between dyadic Agreeableness composition and shared goal achievement, with Homogeneous-Agreeable pairs achieving success 10 times the rate of Homogeneous-Disagreeable pairs (62% vs. 6%). Behavioral mediation analysis reveals that Agreeableness shapes goal achievement partially through cooperative strategy selection, though it continues to predict outcomes within the same dominant strategy, indicating pathways beyond observable conversational behavior. Robustness analyses confirm high consistency of results across repeated simulations (ICC = 0.89) and stable personality expression across diverse scenarios, validating personality anchoring as a viable operationalization strategy.

</details>


### [30] [CANote: Empowering Fact-checking Note Writing Through Scaffolded and Provenance-based Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07101)
*Shuning Zhang,Jingruo Chen,Yuwei Chuai,Dai Shi,Yifan Wang,Xin Yi,Hewu Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: CANote是一个AI辅助的辟谣笔记撰写系统，通过子索赔提取、证据链接和结构化协同起草来提升众包事实核查的质量。实验表明，CANote显著提高了笔记质量，使普通用户能写出与专家相当水平的辟谣笔记，但这也带来了用户所有权和控制感下降的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包事实核查机制（如X平台的社区笔记）对缓解 misinformation 传播至关重要，但撰写高质量、基于证据的辟谣笔记给贡献者带来了沉重负担。为减轻这一负担并提升笔记质量，作者提出了CANote系统。

Method: CANote系统包含三个核心功能：1) 从社交媒体帖子中提取子索赔（subclaim）；2) 通过子索赔与检索到的证据之间的显式链接提供来源追溯；3) 生成中立、结构化的草稿以辅助人类推理。作者在模拟X平台上对52名事实核查专家和52名普通用户进行了评估。

Result: CANote显著提升了笔记质量，尤其使普通用户能写出与专家水平相当的辟谣笔记。任务完成时间和感知认知负荷与手动撰写相当，但用户满意度显著提高。然而，AI辅助也带来了用户所有权和控制感下降的权衡。

Conclusion: CANote通过AI辅助的结构化协同起草有效提升了众包事实核查的笔记质量，降低了普通用户与专家之间的水平差距，但需注意在提升效率的同时保持用户的参与感和控制感。

Abstract: Crowdsourced fact-checking mechanisms, such as X's Community Notes, play a critical role in mitigating the spread of misinformation. However, drafting high-quality, evidence-based debunking notes imposes a substantial burden on contributors. We present CANote, an AI-assisted debunking note writing system featuring evidence correlation and structured co-drafting. CANote scaffolds the workflow by extracting subclaims from social media posts, providing provenance through explicit links between subclaims and retrieved evidence, and generating neutral, structural drafts to support human reasoning. We evaluated CANote against manual writing (N=52 fact-checkers, N=52 lay users) on simulated X platform, where we found CANote significantly improves note quality. Notably, CANote enables lay users to write notes that have comparable quality to those written by experts. While the task completion time and perceived cognitive load remain comparable to manual drafting, CANote significantly increases user satisfaction. However, this assistance introduces a trade-off, resulting in a reduced sense of user ownership and control over the debunking note.

</details>


### [31] [Moodie: An Early-Stage Design Exploration for Supporting Fear of Missing Out with LLM-based Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07231)
*Hsin-Yu Tsai,Jingxian Liao,Fu-Yin Cherng,Tzu-Hsiang Huang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Moodie，一个基于大语言模型的聊天机器人，旨在帮助用户调节情绪并减少错失恐惧症（FoMO）。通过形成性研究和为期一周的初步评估（N=21），发现Moodie与基线聊天机器人（GPT-4o）在减少FoMO方面效果相当，但Moodie带来了更高的用户参与度和社交连接感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未能提供关注FoMO情绪和认知层面的、可访问的交互式工具，因此需要开发专门针对FoMO的情绪调节支持工具。

Method: 先进行形成性研究以了解FoMO用户需求，开发Moodie聊天机器人；随后开展为期一周的初步评估研究（N=21），比较Moodie与基线聊天机器人（GPT-4o）的效果。

Result: Moodie与GPT-4o在减少FoMO方面效果相似，但Moodie在用户参与度和社交连接感方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 针对特定心理健康问题设计的专用聊天机器人相比通用模型具有优势，未来需进行聊天日志分析、原型优化和纵向评估。

Abstract: The excessive use of social media has led to the challenge known as Fear of Missing Out (FoMO). Existing studies fail to provide accessible, interactive tools that focus on the emotional and cognitive aspects of FoMO. This work presents Moodie, a chatbot designed using Large Language Models to support emotion regulation and reduce FoMO. We conducted a formative study to understand the needs of individuals with FoMO and developed Moodie. Then, we conducted a preliminary evaluative study (N=21) to observe how participants interact with Moodie and a baseline chatbot (GPT-4o) over one week. The results show that while both Moodie and a baseline chatbot reduced FoMO to a similar extent, Moodie resulted in greater engagement and social connection. This finding raises interesting questions about the advantages of purpose-built chatbots compared to general-purpose models for mental health support. Future research will include chat log analysis, prototype refinements, and longitudinal evaluations.

</details>


### [32] [A Model of Integrated Information Processing in Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07283)
*Tim Schrills. Thomas Franke*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了集成信息处理（IIP）模型，这是一个以任务为中心、基于控制论的人-AI交互模型，通过统一人类和机器的建模语言，用三种信息处理质量（输入充分性、参考一致性、输出可操作性）来刻画人机耦合的有效性，为接口设计和评估提供理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HAII研究缺乏一个从心理学视角出发、以任务为中心且面向过程的统一理论框架，无法将行为调节机制与具体的接口设计与评估杠杆相连接，也缺乏统一的人类与机器描述语言。

Method: 基于控制论，将人类、机器及其联合活动概念化为耦合控制回路，提出包含输入充分性、参考一致性和输出可操作性三种集成质量的IIP模型，并使用统一建模语言描述人类和人工智能体。

Result: IIP模型将接口选择（如XAI技术）映射到理论驱动的用户行为预期，指导接口设计与评估；并通过三个示范用例展示了模型在设计中的应用含义。

Conclusion: IIP模型通过三种信息处理质量将心理机制与接口设计联系起来，为透明性和可控性等人本中心基准提供了理论依据，是现有HCI和自动化研究的延伸而非替代。

Abstract: For Human-AI Interaction (HAII) research to move forward, theoretical work linking psychological mechanisms to interface design is needed. Such work should extend rather than replace established HCI and automation research, adapting to the increasing autonomy and agency of AI systems. Building on prior frameworks focused on roles and levels in human interaction with automation, a gap remains from a psychological view: a task-centered, process-oriented account that links mechanisms of action regulation to concrete design and evaluation levers for human-AI coupling, expressed in a unified vocabulary for human and machine. Moreover, existing models may describe how a system is designed (e.g., function allocation in automation) but fall short in showing how this design affects human behavior. We present the Integrated Information Processing (IIP) model, a task-centered, cybernetic model that conceptualizes humans, machines, and their joint activity as coupled control loops. The IIP model uses a unified modeling language for human and artificial agents, making psychological models of action regulation accessible for AI system design. As a core feature, we argue that efficacy within a shared task is characterized by three integration qualities, input adequacy, reference consonance, and output operativity, which critically influence benchmarks of human-centeredness such as transparency and controllability. The model maps interface choices (e.g., XAI techniques) to theory-driven expectations of user behavior, guiding interface design and evaluation. To this end, we present (1) a continuity-preserving theoretical discourse that extends HAII to agency in AI; (2) the IIP model with three information-processing qualities; and (3) applications of the IIP model to exemplary use cases demonstrating implications for interface design.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [33] [Robots Need More than VLA and World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06556)
*Elis Karcini,Faisal Mehrban,Quang Nguyen,Mac Schwager,Arash Ajoudani,Cesar Cadena,Jan Peters,Marco Hutter,Haitham Bou-Ammar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出通用机器人智能发展的瓶颈不仅在于策略扩展，更在于缺乏将海量非结构化行为数据转化为机器人可用的监督信号的机制。论文识别了四个缺失组件：数据自动标注接口、人体运动重定向接口、物理世界模型接口和奖励推断接口，并提出了一个使机器人能从更广泛的物理世界学习的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前通用机器人智能被简化为策略扩展问题（收集更多演示、训练更大VLA模型），但这种方法忽略了非结构化行为数据（人类运动、互联网视频、仿真数据等）无法直接被机器人策略利用的根本问题，因为这些数据缺少具体化的动作标签、任务语义和奖励结构。

Method: 本文采用立场论文形式，通过识别四个缺失组件（数据接口、具身接口、世界模型接口、奖励接口），并综述机器人基础模型、跨具身数据集、视频学习、世界模型和奖励建模等领域的近期进展，提出研究议程。

Result: 识别了限制机器人利用通用行为数据的四个关键瓶颈：1) 非结构化行为的自动标注接口；2) 人体运动到机器人动作的重定向接口；3) 基于物理的3D推理世界模型接口；4) 从视频和语言推断任务进度与成功的奖励接口。

Conclusion: 机器人系统不应仅从机器人演示中学习，而应能从更广泛的物理世界中学习，这需要构建上述四个接口组件来弥合非结构化行为数据与机器人可用监督信号之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Generalist robot intelligence is often framed as a policy-scaling problem: collect more robot demonstrations, train larger Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, and expect broader generalisation. In this position paper, we argue that this framing is incomplete. The central bottleneck is not only policy learning, but the absence of mechanisms that convert the world's abundant unstructured behavioural data into grounded robot supervision. Human motion, internet video, simulation rollouts, and interactive demonstrations contain rich information about tasks, goals, contacts, failures, and physical constraints, yet most of this information is not directly usable by robot policies because it lacks embodiment-specific action labels, task semantics, and reward structure. We identify four missing components for the next generation of robotics: data interfaces for autolabelling unstructured behaviour, embodiment interfaces for retargeting human motion to robot actions, world-model interfaces for physics-grounded 3D reasoning, and reward interfaces for inferring task progress and success from video and language. We survey recent progress in robot foundation models, cross-embodiment datasets, learning from video, world models, and reward modelling, and propose a research agenda for building robotics systems that can learn not only from robot demonstrations, but from the broader physical world.

</details>


### [34] [PhyRoGen: Synthetic Generation of Physical Robot Manipulation Puzzles Using Procedural Content Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06569)
*Lennart Julian Droß,Andreas Orthey,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PhyRoGen框架，利用程序化内容生成技术自动创建机器人操作谜题的合成数据集，可生成24种具有互锁物体依赖关系的物理谜题，并在物理仿真中用KUKA机器人验证了所有谜题的可操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人解决物理谜题需要大量训练数据，而手动生成这些数据集耗时且繁琐，因此需要自动化生成方法。

Method: 提出PhyRoGen框架，基于程序化内容生成（PCG）自动生成具有互锁物体依赖关系的操作谜题合成数据集，并定义了6个具体生成器来生成24种物理谜题。

Result: 所有24种谜题可在1-300秒内通过基于采样的规划算法求解，且在KUKA LBR iiwa机器人物理仿真中验证了每个生成谜题的可操作性。

Conclusion: PhyRoGen能够程序化生成独特、可解的机器人操作谜题，为操作算法基准测试和鲁棒基础模型开发提供了关键工具。

Abstract: Robot manipulation of physical puzzles is important for automatic assembly and disassembly tasks. However, to enable robots to solve physical puzzles, manipulation skills need to be learned, which requires large training datasets, the generation of which is often time consuming and tedious. To overcome this problem, we propose the Physical Robot Manipulation Puzzle Generation framework (PhyRoGen), which leverages procedural content generation (PCG) for automated generation of synthetic datasets of manipulation puzzles. PhyRoGen is a general-purpose puzzle generator, which can generate physical puzzles with interlocking object dependencies, where one articulated object must be manipulated before another can be moved. Based upon PhyRoGen, we define six concrete generators which we use to generate 24 physical puzzles. By using a benchmarking framework, we are able to solve all puzzles in 1 to 300 seconds using sampling-based planning algorithms. Finally, we demonstrate that every generated puzzle is manipulatable by using a KUKA LBR iiwa robot in a physical simulation. This shows that our framework is able to procedurally generate unique, solvable robot manipulation puzzles, which is a crucial ingredient to benchmark manipulation algorithms and to develop robust foundation models.

Abstract: How can we plan long-horizon routes that reach designated goals, visit required waypoints, and remain short when only short-horizon offline trajectories are available? This problem matters in offline navigation because collecting sufficiently rich long-horizon data is difficult, yet real agents must still solve long-range tasks with route-level efficiency rather than mere feasibility. The difficulty is twofold: at the microscopic level, composing many short-horizon segments creates a trade-off between search cost and path quality, while at the macroscopic level, waypoint ordering requires comparing pairwise travel costs among start, goal, and waypoint anchors that are unknown before planning and increasingly unreliable when estimated only from long-range temporal distance.
  In this paper, we propose ChronoForest, a closed-loop planning system that couples local bridge search and online route re-solving through an anchor-chaining tree diffusion planner and an online multi-tree orchestrator. ChronoForest uses temporal distance for short-range guidance and node evaluation, while using search-time bridge evidence to validate long-range anchor connectivity and repeatedly re-solve the route. On OGBench AntMaze-Stitch, ChronoForest achieves 99.8%, 99.3%, and 99.5% success on the medium, large, and giant splits and improves giant-stitch success by up to 34.5 points over prior reported diffusion-based results. On Hamiltonian route-composition benchmarks, online re-solving corrects poor temporal orderings and improves route quality while remaining substantially cheaper than exhaustive planning.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主CPS处于MBSE与数据驱动ML/AI的交叉点，但缺乏原生涵盖两者的集成系统工程方法论。

Method: 提出IDDMBSE方法论，扩展MBSE V流程，在每个阶段加入数据驱动循环；基于SysML、自主栈和混合模型驱动+数据驱动折衷架构；实现为PERFECT（SysML到ROS映射）、TRADES-X（设计空间分解）和VERITAS（形式化+数据驱动+运行时验证）工具链。

Abstract: Autonomous cyber-physical systems (CPS) sit at the intersection of Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) and data-driven Machine Learning and Artificial Intelligence (ML/AI), yet no integrated Systems Engineering (SE) methodology natively spans both. We address this gap with IDDMBSE, an Integrated Data-Driven and Model-Based Systems Engineering methodology that extends the rigorous MBSE V-process with a data-driven loop at every step, anchored in SysML, the autonomy stack, and a hybrid model-based plus data-driven trade-off architecture. We instantiate IDDMBSE as an interoperable, open-source tool chain: PERFECT, which maps SysML system architectures to executable ROS autonomy stacks for scalable performance evaluation; TRADES-X, which decomposes design-space exploration into a model-based optimization stage followed by a data-driven evaluation stage; and VERITAS, which combines formal, data-driven, and runtime verification into a single assurance workflow. We demonstrate IDDMBSE on a Trusted Autonomous Ground Robot across its development lifecycle, spanning sensor-suite selection, risk-sensitive path planning, behavior-tree task verification, conformal-prediction-based robust perception, and assured multi-robot coordination, all exercised in a contested-terrain Isaac Sim test range that we release with the tool chain. We close by sketching how IDDMBSE is being re-formulated on SysML v2 / KerML foundations to enable language-native composability and tighter ML/AI integration.

</details>

Method: 构建了一个包含532个人类视频、28小时高质量三角化手部标注的自然运动数据集；通过对比实验分析手部姿态质量对迁移的影响；提出视觉和策略网络针对不同实体（人类vs机器人）进行专门化的协同训练方案。

Conclusion: 互联网日常视频可用于机器人操作策略的协同训练，但需要针对手部姿态质量和网络专门化进行设计；提出的协同训练策略在低数据场景下效果显著。

Abstract: Human video datasets used for cotraining robot manipulation policies largely consist of curated demonstrations where motions are orchestrated to resemble robot behavior and 3D hand poses are captured with specialized hardware. A more plentiful source of data is everyday Internet video, but it is an open question what factors enable transfer from such videos to robots. We investigate this using a new dataset of 532 human videos with 28 hours of high-quality triangulated hand labels and natural motions. We find that hand pose quality affects transfer, but even with accurate hands, the inherent motion gap hinders transfer unless the vision and policy networks specialize to each embodiment. Our cotraining recipe yields consistent improvements, with an absolute success rate gain of $29.7\%$ in the low-robot-data regime across six manipulation tasks.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出ERNEST行星车概念，配备主动万向节悬挂系统，通过单个神经网络控制器实现自主越障。该控制器使用强化学习在DARTS仿真中训练，通过策略合并实现在异构地形上的统一控制，并通过域随机化实现零样本迁移到物理样机。实验证明其在岩石地、沙坡等挑战性地形上的优越性能，在20°干沙坡上运输成本降低37%。
