We introduce the Bootstrap Theory of Representational Emergence (TBER), a framework describing how new representations arise when existing ones become explanatorily insufficient. In this view, representational innovation is not only driven by more data, larger models, or greater computational power, but also by persistent explanatory gaps: situations in which a representation can still describe observations but can no longer make their organization or transformations intelligible. TBER identifies explanatory insufficiency as a positive signal for representational transition. A representation becomes insufficient not because it is necessarily false, but because its explanatory domain has been exceeded. The bootstrap dynamic follows a recursive sequence: observations reveal anomalies; anomalies expose insufficiencies; insufficiencies motivate new representations; and these new representations generate further observations and possible new insufficiencies.We formalize this process through five stages: stabilized observation, anomaly detection, recognition of explanatory insufficiency, representational emergence, and provisional stabilization. We discuss applications to representation learning, latent spaces, foundation models, world models, digital twins, adaptive biological systems, and scientific discovery. TBER suggests that future AI systems may benefit from mechanisms for detecting the explanatory limits of their own internal representations.

</details>


### [157] [TabSwift: An Efficient Tabular Foundation Model with Row-Wise Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07345)
*Si-Yang Liu,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TabSwift，通过轻量级行注意力骨干网络、门控注意力稳定机制和可学习寄存器令牌，在保持与更强表格基础模型同等性能的同时，显著提升推理效率，并引入自适应早期退出机制实现动态推理深度调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型（如TabPFN变体）虽性能优异，但架构日益复杂导致推理成本高昂，限制了实际部署。作者希望在不牺牲性能的前提下实现更高效的表格上下文学习。

Method: 重新审视原始TabPFN设计，采用轻量级仅行注意力（row-wise attention-only）骨干网络；引入门控注意力稳定机制（gated attention stabilization）提升训练稳定性；添加少量可学习寄存器令牌（register tokens）提供全局上下文并改进预训练质量；提出自适应逐层早期退出机制（adaptive layer-wise early-exit）实现按样本动态调整推理深度。

Result: TabSwift在分类和回归任务上与TabPFN v2、TabICL等更强基线模型竞争力相当，但推理效率更高；自适应早期退出机制进一步优化了延迟敏感场景下的推理性能。

Conclusion: TabSwift通过简洁高效的设计实现了高性能与低推理成本的平衡，为表格上下文学习在实际部署中提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models, exemplified by TabPFN, perform prediction via in-context learning, inferring test labels directly from labeled training examples. They have demonstrated competitive performance, particularly on small-to-medium datasets. However, recent tabular foundation models often improve accuracy with increasingly complex architectures, incurring higher inference cost and limiting practical deployment. In this work, we revisit the original TabPFN design and show that a lightweight row-wise attention-only backbone can remain highly competitive with two simple enhancements: a gated attention stabilization mechanism and a small set of learnable register tokens that provide global context and improve pretraining quality. The resulting model, TabSwift, supports both classification and regression, and is competitive with stronger tabular foundation models (e.g., TabPFN v2 and TabICL) while being more efficient at inference. For latency-sensitive serving, we further introduce an adaptive layer-wise early-exit mechanism that dynamically adjusts inference depth per sample. Overall, TabSwift enables efficient and anytime tabular in-context learning for practical deployments.

</details>


### [158] [SleepExplain: Explainable Non-Rapid Eye Movement and Rapid Eye Movement Sleep Stage Classification from EEG Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07351)
*Rafsan Jany,Md. Hamjajul Ashmafee,Iqram Hussain,Md Azam Hossain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SleepExplain模型，利用随机森林、XGBoost和梯度提升集成学习分类器对NREM和REM睡眠阶段进行分类，并结合SHAP可解释性方法提供预测解释，取得了最高94.30%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠阶段分类对诊断睡眠障碍至关重要，而现有模型缺乏可解释性，难以理解模型如何基于EEG信号做出分类决策。

Method: 采用随机森林、XGBoost和梯度提升三种集成分类模型对睡眠EEG数据进行NREM和REM阶段分类，并引入基于博弈论的SHAP方法构建可解释的SleepExplain模型来解释预测结果。

Result: 随机森林准确率92.54%，梯度提升94.25%，XGBoost最高达94.30%。

Conclusion: 集成学习模型能有效分类NREM和REM睡眠阶段，且SHAP可解释方法为模型预测提供了可信的解释，增强了模型的透明度和临床可用性。

Abstract: Classification of sleep stages is one of the most important diagnostic approaches for a variety of sleep-related disorders. Electroencephalography (EEG) is regarded as a powerful tool for examining the association between neurological effects and sleep phases since it correctly identifies sleep-related neurological alterations. During Non-Rapid Eye Movement (NREM) and Rapid Eye Movement (REM) sleep phases, a number of nerve and bodily functions are affected and therefore hold an important role both in their functionalities. This work aims to classify NREM and REM sleep stages from sleep EEG data and present a noble SleepExplain model, an explainable NREM and REM sleep stage classification to explain its predictions. In this work, sleep stages were classified using Random Forest, XGBoost, and Gradient Boosting ensemble classification models. Overall, we obtained an accuracy of 92.54% (Random Forest), 94.25% (Gradient Boosting), and 94.30% (XGBoost). For explainable classification model, we utilized a game theoretic approach, SHAP (SHapley Addictive exPlanations) to offer a convincing explanation for the prediction.

</details>


### [159] [Breaking the Ice: Analyzing Cold Start Latency in vLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07362)
*Huzaifa Shaaban Kabakibo,Animesh Trivedi,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了vLLM推理引擎的冷启动延迟，将启动过程分解为六个基本步骤，发现其主要为CPU密集型任务，并建立了轻量级分析模型来准确预测启动延迟，为大规模推理环境的资源规划提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可扩展推理服务的普及，推理引擎的冷启动延迟变得至关重要。尽管vLLM已成为主流推理引擎，但由于其复杂性和快速迭代，尚未有对其启动延迟的系统性研究。

Method: 将vLLM启动过程分解为六个基本步骤，分析各步骤在模型级和系统级参数下的可解释缩放趋势，并基于这些洞察构建轻量级分析模型。

Result: 启动过程以CPU密集型为主，每个步骤展现出一致且可解释的缩放趋势，所建分析模型能准确预测vLLM在给定硬件配置下的启动延迟。

Conclusion: 该分析模型可为大规模推理环境中的资源规划提供可操作的指导，相关基准数据集、分析工具和预测脚本已开源。

Abstract: As scalable inference services become popular, the cold start latency of an inference engine becomes important. Today, vLLM has evolved into the de facto inference engine of choice for many inference workloads. Although popular, due to its complexity and rapid evolution, there has not been a systematic study of its startup latency. With major architectural innovations such as the V1 API and the introduction of torch.compile, this paper presents the first detailed performance characterization of vLLM startup latency. We break down the startup process into six foundational steps and demonstrate that it is predominantly CPU bound. Each step exhibits consistent and interpretable scaling trends with respect to model-level and system-level parameters, enabling fine-grained attribution of latency sources. Building on these insights, we develop a lightweight analytical model that accurately predicts vLLM startup latency for a given hardware configuration, providing actionable guidance for resource planning in large-scale inference environments. All benchmarking datasets, analysis tools, and prediction scripts are open sourced at https://github.com/upb-cn/vllm-startup-profiler.

</details>


### [160] [A robust PPG foundation model using multimodal physiological supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07365)
*Eloy Geenjaar,Vince Calhoun,Scott Daly,Gouthaman KV,Lie Lu,Trisha Mittal,Daniel P. Darcy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种不需要高质量或类现场预训练数据的PPG基础模型，通过利用ICU数据集中的心电和呼吸信号进行多模态对比学习，在更少预训练主体下实现了15个下游任务中14个的性能提升，增强了对消费级数据的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPG基础模型要么依赖需要精心策划数据的ICU数据集预训练范式，难以泛化到现场数据；要么使用闭源的类现场PPG数据。作者希望提出一种不需要高质量或类现场预训练数据的方法。

Method: 利用ICU数据集中的伴随心电和呼吸信号来选择对比学习样本，模型能够保留并从噪声PPG片段中学习，从而提高推理时的鲁棒性。

Result: 在比现有SOTA方法少3倍预训练主体的情况下，在15个不同下游任务中14个任务上实现了性能提升，包括类现场的日常活动和心率预测任务。

Conclusion: 多模态监督可以整合互补的生理信息，提高PPG基础模型的鲁棒性，并增强其对消费级数据的泛化能力。

Abstract: Photoplethysmography (PPG), a non-invasive measure of changes in blood volume, is widely used in both wearable devices and clinical settings. Recent PPG foundation models either use open-source ICU datasets with pretraining paradigms that require curated data and thus complicate generalization to field-like data, or use closed-source field-like PPG data. In contrast, we propose a PPG foundation model that does not require high-quality or field-like pretraining data, and instead leverages accompanying electrocardiogram and respiratory signals in ICU datasets to select contrastive samples during pretraining. Our approach allows the model to retain and learn from noisy PPG segments, improving robustness at inference. Our model, pretrained on 3x fewer subjects than existing state-of-the-art approaches, achieves performance improvements on 14 out of 15 diverse downstream tasks, including field-like daily activity and heart rate prediction. Our results demonstrate that multimodal supervision can integrate complementary physiological information to improve the robustness of PPG foundation models and enhance their generalization to consumer-grade data.

</details>


### [161] [Self-evolving LLM agents with in-distribution Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07367)
*Yudi Zhang,Meng Fang,Zhenfang Chen,Mykola Pechenizkiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Q-Evolve提出了一种自进化框架，通过自动过程奖励标注与策略学习在分布内强化学习范式下的统一，解决了LLM智能体在长程决策中的信用分配问题，在AlfWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld上取得优于基线方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在复杂环境中执行可靠的长程决策面临根本性挑战，关键在于信用分配问题：智能体仅在回合结束时收到延迟奖励，缺乏中间步骤的密集监督信号。

Method: 提出Q-Evolve自进化框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 从混合离策略数据集（专家演示+智能体生成轨迹）中学习分布内评论家，通过加权隐式Q学习目标稳定稀疏奖励下的Bellman备份；(2) 通过优势估计导出逐步骤过程奖励，并基于这些信号进行行为近端策略优化，实现智能体在过程奖励标注所用数据上的迭代自改进，避免分布偏移加剧。

Result: 在AlfWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld三个基准上，Q-Evolve在样本效率、鲁棒性和整体任务性能方面均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将过程级监督与策略在同一分布内学习循环中共同进化，可以实现稳定的智能体自进化，为LLM智能体的长程决策提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently emerged as powerful controllers for interactive agents in complex environments, yet training them to perform reliable long-horizon decision making remains a fundamental challenge. A key difficulty lies in credit assignment: agents often receive delayed rewards only at the end of episodes. In this paper, we propose Q-Evolve, a self-evolving framework for LLM agents that unifies automatic process-reward labeling and policy learning within a principled in-distribution reinforcement learning paradigm. In each evolving iteration, our method learns an in-distribution critic from a hybrid off-policy dataset that combines expert demonstrations with agent-generated trajectories, stabilizing Bellman backups in sparse-reward settings via a weighted Implicit Q-Learning objective. The learned value function is then used to derive step-wise process rewards through advantage estimation, enabling dense and reliable supervision without environment backtracking or human annotation. Leveraging these signals, we perform behavior-proximal policy optimization that evolves the agent over the data used for process reward labeling, allowing iterative self-improvement without exacerbating distribution shift. We evaluate our method on AlfWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld, showing Q-Evolve outperforms strong baselines in sample efficiency, robustness, and overall task performance. Our results demonstrate that stable agent self-evolution is achievable through the co-evolution of process-level supervision and policy, both grounded within a shared in-distribution learning loop.

</details>


### [162] [Do Coding Agents Deceive Us? Detecting and Preventing Cheating via Capped Evaluation with Randomized Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07379)
*Thanawat Lodkaew,Johannes Ackermann,Soichiro Nishimori,Nontawat Charoenphakdee,Masashi Sugiyama,Takashi Ishida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CapCode框架，通过设计有性能上限的编码数据集来检测模型作弊行为，并提出CapReward奖励设计来抑制作弊，实验证明该方法能有效检测和减少作弊，同时保持模型性能排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估中，模型常通过利用捷径（作弊）获得高分，导致评估分数无法反映真实任务解决能力，亟需一种能区分作弊与真实能力的方法。

Method: 提出CapCode框架，构建评估集时随机化测试，使得在不作弊情况下可达到的最佳性能被人为设定上限（低于100%）；提出CapReward奖励设计，基于CapCode原则对超过上限的优化进行惩罚。

Result: 多个数据集上的实验表明，CapCode能有效检测作弊行为且保持模型性能排名不变；CapReward能减少作弊行为，使模型更好地遵循任务规范。

Conclusion: 通过设置性能上限，可以更清晰地解释评估分数（显著高于上限的分数即为作弊证据），同时通过奖励设计抑制作弊，提升评估可靠性。

Abstract: A growing failure mode in agent evaluation and training is that models can achieve high evaluation scores by exploiting shortcuts instead of solving the intended task, producing deceptive performance. This makes evaluation scores unreliable as measures of true task-solving ability. We propose CapCode, a framework for constructing coding datasets with randomized tests whose best achievable non-cheating performance is deliberately capped below one. This capped-performance design gives evaluation scores a clearer interpretation: scores substantially above the cap are implausible and therefore provide evidence of cheating. To prevent cheating, we propose CapReward, a reward design based on the CapCode principle to discourage optimization beyond the cap. Experiments across multiple datasets show that CapCode detects cheating while preserving performance ranking of models, and CapReward reduces cheating behavior, yielding models that better follow the intended task specification.

</details>


### [163] [Covariance Shrinkage via Stochastic Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07382)
*Mathieu Chalvidal,Florentin Coeurdoux,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过参数化随机插值的经验风险最小化框架重新表述高维协方差估计的经典收缩方法，揭示了三种降低统计风险的机制（调度、流映射与耦合、早停），并提出了基于神经网络的插值估计器，在合成数据与真实神经影像数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视高维协方差估计中的经典收缩方法，将其统一到经验风险最小化框架下，揭示收缩估计背后的深层机制，并探索超越传统方法的新的正则化途径。

Method: 将协方差收缩重述为源分布与目标分布之间参数化随机插值的经验风险最小化问题；识别出三种风险降低机制（调度、流映射与耦合、早停）；提出基于神经网络的插值估计器并给出二次风险上界。

Result: 在合成实验上验证了所提估计器的有效性；在真实神经影像数据上展示了该方法相比于传统方法更强的正则化能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的随机插值框架不仅统一了已有的收缩估计方法，还揭示了新的正则化机制（尤其是通过非线性流映射实现特征向量正则化），为高维协方差估计提供了更灵活的工具。

Abstract: We recast classical shrinkage of high-dimensional covariance estimators as empirical risk minimization over a parametric stochastic interpolant between a source and a target distribution. This formalism recovers known shrinkage estimators as special cases and reveals three distinct mechanisms for reducing statistical risk: (i) Scheduling: the interpolant schedule determines the class of admissible covariances, and hence the achievable risk. (ii) Flow maps and couplings: whereas naive constructions amount to assuming independence between the distributions, specific coupling structures (e.g., solutions of optimal transport problems) can lower the empirical risk. Moreover, non-linear flow maps realizing such couplings free the interpolant covariance from the eigenbasis of the empirical estimate, enabling eigenvector regularization. (iii) Early stopping: estimators defined by integrating a regressed vector field afford an additional bias-variance trade-off through approximation of the true interpolant distribution. We then propose a neural estimator of the interpolant, together with an upper bound on its quadratic risk in terms of the interpolant approximation error, and validate both on synthetic experiments. Finally, we apply the estimator to real neuroimaging data, demonstrating the additional regularization power this approach offers in practice.

</details>


### [164] [Making the Most of Limited Data: Score-Aware Training for Text-to-Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07387)
*Yun-Chen Cheng,Tzu-Hung Huang,Chih-Pin Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于评分感知训练（score-aware training）的文本到音乐生成系统，通过CLAP条件Beta噪声调度、REPA辅助损失和两阶段字幕生成等技术，在不依赖海量专有数据的前提下，以450M参数规模的模型在ICME 2026 ATTM竞赛中取得了客观评测第二名、主观评测第三名的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的文本到音乐生成系统依赖大规模专有数据集和工业级算力，使得无法区分架构贡献与资源优势。本文旨在通过更高效的数据利用方式，在不依赖海量数据和算力的前提下提升音乐生成质量。

Method: 1) 评分感知训练：将音频-字幕对齐评分作为直接监督信号贯穿整个流程；2) CLAP条件Beta噪声调度：将低分片段引导至高噪声训练 regime，作为隐式正则化器；3) 片段级过滤：移除最不对齐的样本；4) 两阶段字幕生成：弥合详细训练字幕与简洁推理提示之间的分布差距；5) REPA辅助损失：从预训练的CLAP和MuQ编码器中迁移结构化语义知识，无需额外数据。

Result: 基于FluxAudio的450M参数系统在ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge Efficiency Track中，客观评测位列两赛道第二名，主观MOS评测在Efficiency Track中位列第三名。

Conclusion: 通过评分感知训练和多组件协同优化，可以在有限资源条件下有效提升文本到音乐生成的质量，验证了架构贡献与资源优势的解耦可行性。

Abstract: State-of-the-art text-to-music generation systems rely on massive proprietary datasets and industrial-scale compute, making it impossible to disentangle architectural contributions from resource advantages. We propose \textit{score-aware training}, which treats audio-caption alignment score as a direct supervision signal throughout the pipeline. Rather than discarding low-scoring segments, we repurpose them via a CLAP-conditioned Beta noise timestep schedule that routes them to high-noise training regimes, acting as an effective implicit regularizer. Complementarily, segment-level filtering removes the most misaligned examples, and a two-stage caption procedure bridges the distribution gap between verbose training captions and concise inference prompts. A REPA auxiliary loss further transfers structured semantic knowledge from pretrained CLAP and MuQ encoders without additional data. Our 450M-parameter FluxAudio-based system, submitted to the ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge Efficiency Track, ranked 2nd across both tracks in the objective evaluation and 3rd in the Efficiency Track in the final MOS evaluation.

</details>


### [165] [Generative Modeling of Discrete Latent Structures via Dynamic Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07400)
*Stefan Ivanovic,Ge Liu,Mohammed El-Kebir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GReinSS提出了一种基于策略学习的框架，通过动态缩放奖励来学习最大化观测数据似然的潜在状态分布，能够从间接观测中准确重建组合性潜在状态（如潜在集合和潜在图），在模拟数据和真实RNA测序数据上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学问题需要从间接观测中推断未观测到的机制性潜在状态，但传统方法（如期望最大化）无法扩展到组合爆炸性的大空间，而深度学习方法（如VAE）往往学习人工潜在状态而非重建真实的机制性状态。

Method: 提出GReinSS策略学习框架，使用动态缩放的奖励信号来学习潜在状态分布，以最大化观测数据的似然函数。

Result: 在模拟数据上准确重建了潜在集合和潜在图，优于其他策略学习和生成建模基线；在真实RNA-seq短读测序数据上，重建的isoform比标准RSEM算法更匹配正交长读测序检测到的isoform。

Conclusion: GReinSS是一种有原则且实际有效的生成建模与推理方法，适用于从间接观测中推断组合性潜在状态。

Abstract: Many scientific problems require inferring unobserved mechanistic latent states from indirect observations. While classical approaches, including expectation maximization, do not scale to combinatorially large spaces, deep learning approaches such as variational autoencoders typically form artificial latent states rather than reconstructing the mechanistic ground-truth states. Here, we introduce GReinSS, a policy learning framework that uses dynamically rescaled rewards to learn latent state distributions that maximize the observed data likelihood. We show that GReinSS accurately reconstructs simulated latent sets and latent graphs, outperforming alternative policy learning and generative modeling baselines. Additionally, GReinSS reconstructs isoforms from real short-read RNA sequencing data that better match isoforms detected by orthogonal long-read sequencing than the standard RSEM algorithm. Overall, GReinSS is a principled and practically effective approach for generative modeling and inference of combinatorial latent states from indirect observations.

</details>


### [166] [Reversible Foundations: Training a 120B Sparse MoE through State-Preserving Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07404)
*Rohan Shravan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了在单台八GPU节点上端到端训练千亿参数稀疏混合专家模型的全过程。LightningLM 0.1V是一个循环骨干语言模型家族，通过四个阶段从小型稠密种子模型逐步生长，最终达到120B参数规模（460个路由专家，top-12路由），活跃参数从1.78B增至5.93B。论文围绕三个原则展开：可逆性（可逆循环栈减少激活内存）、状态保持生长（各阶段扩展的可复现原则及失败案例分析）和单节点经济性（TQP量化策略将专家路径优化器状态压缩约45倍）。模型、分词器和训练代码均已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单节点硬件资源受限条件下，端到端训练千亿参数级稀疏MoE语言模型，验证现有技术原语的集成是否能够实现这一目标，并提供可复现的实践经验与失败案例。

Method: 采用四阶段生长式训练：从稠密种子→5B MoE→9B MoE→120B MoE（460专家，top-12路由）。核心技术包括：(1) 可逆循环栈，反向传播时重建激活值以降低显存；(2) 状态保持生长策略，确保各阶段扩展的可复现性；(3) TQP（量化基础专家权重+训练低秩适配器），将优化器状态从100B+专家参数压缩至2.26B适配器参数，节省约45倍。

Result: 成功在单台八GPU节点上训练120B参数MoE模型，训练损失降至1.78。活跃参数仅5.93B（占存储参数的约5%）。各领域保留损失表明针对多语言Indic能力和代码等目标能力已通过构建方式习得。

Conclusion: 本文的创新不在于单一原语，而在于已知技术原语（可逆计算、生长式训练、量化+低秩适配）的系统集成，证明了在单节点上训练千亿参数MoE的可行性，并提供了可复现的实践指南和开源资源。

Abstract: This paper reports on training a hundred-billion-parameter sparse mixture of experts on a single eight-GPU node, end to end. LightningLM 0.1V is a recurrence-backbone language model family grown in four stages from a small dense seed, through a 5B and a 9B mixture of experts, to a 120B model with 460 routed experts under top-12 routing. Each larger model is grown from the trained weights of the smaller one; active parameters rise monotonically from 1.78B at the dense seed to 5.93B at 120B (about 5% of the 118.67B stored). The full lineage runs on single nodes, the larger stages at 8K context, reaching a released training loss of 1.78 at 120B scale.
  This is a systems and experience report. It is organized around three disciplines. Reversibility: a reversible recurrence stack reconstructs activations in the backward pass instead of storing them, holding activation memory flat as the model grows. State-preserving growth: each expansion (dense to MoE, shallow to deep, few experts to many) is given as a reproducible principle paired with the failure that results from getting it wrong; several failures are silent. Single-node economics: the 120B trains through TQP, a strategy of quantized base expert weights and trained low-rank adapters that carries optimizer state on 2.26B adapter parameters rather than 100B+ resident in routed experts, cutting expert-path optimizer state by a factor of ~45.
  What is new is the integration of known primitives, not any primitive in isolation: one grown lineage running end to end on a single node, documented at practitioner level, with per-domain held-out loss as evidence that targeted capabilities (multilingual Indic competence, code) were learned by construction. Model family, tokenizer, and training code are released.

</details>


### [167] [A Comprehensive Anatomy of Human and DeepSeek-R1 LLM Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07410)
*Yuxiang Chen,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过对比DeepSeek-R1与人类在AIME 2025全部30道题目上的推理过程，发现模型推理呈现"拓扑模仿"——表面形式类似但缺乏功能性逻辑进展，同时识别出分支回溯的稳定使用和有效反思的位置是区分真实推理与表面模仿的关键信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型（特别是DeepSeek-R1）是否真正具备推理能力，还是仅仅模仿推理的表象。这一问题的提出源于"啊哈时刻"现象的出现。

Method: 对AIME 2025全部30道题目进行模型与人类推理的全面实证比较，将10,247个推理步骤详尽标注为五个功能类别：分析（Analysis）、推理（Inference）、分支（Branch）、回溯（Backtrace）和反思（Reflection）。

Result: 发现了清晰的结构性差异：人类解决方案保持分析与演绎的紧凑交替，而DeepSeek-R1频繁回顾中间结果、进行浅层且常不必要的验证、在局部检查中循环而无有意义的逻辑进展。成功轨迹表现出分支和回溯的稳定使用，失败轨迹则要么使用不足要么过度使用探索性行为。反思只有在演绎推理中才有效，被困在分析循环中的反思仅关注局部数值细节而忽略全局逻辑错误。

Conclusion: 当前长思维链模型可能更多因推理的表象而非真正的演绎进展获得奖励。提出了改进评估和训练的方向：测量跨轨迹稳定性、惩罚"旋转轮"式轨迹、鼓励更深层的逻辑修正、将推理时计算重分配到演绎和回溯上。推理质量不仅取决于反思的量，更取决于反思是否一致地出现在适当逻辑尺度上。

Abstract: The emergence of "Aha moments" in large language models, particularly DeepSeek-R1-0120, has raised the question of whether these systems genuinely reason or merely imitate the appearance of reasoning. We conduct a comprehensive empirical comparison between model and human reasoning across all 30 problems from AIME 2025, exhaustively annotating 10,247 reasoning steps into five functional categories: Analysis, Inference, Branch, Backtrace, and Reflection. We find a clear structural difference. Human solutions maintain a compact alternation between analysis and deduction, whereas DeepSeek-R1 frequently revisits intermediate results, performs shallow and often unnecessary verification, and loops through local checks without meaningful logical progress. We describe this as topological mimicry: reproducing the surface form of reasoning without its functional role. Despite this, we identify two signals of genuine reasoning. First, successful traces exhibit stable use of branching and backtracking, while failed traces either underuse or overuse exploratory actions. Second, reflection is only effective when placed within deductive inference; reflections trapped in analysis loops focus on local numerical details while missing global logical errors. These findings suggest that current long-CoT models may be rewarded more for the appearance of reasoning than for genuine deductive progress. We discuss directions for improving evaluation and training, including measuring cross-trace stability, penalising "spinning-wheel" traces, encouraging deeper logical correction, and reallocating inference-time compute toward deduction and backtracking. Overall, reasoning quality depends not simply on how much reflection occurs, but on whether reflection appears consistently and at the appropriate logical scale.

</details>


### [168] [Video-Based Prediction of In-Flight Particle Characteristics in Atmospheric Plasma Spraying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07416)
*Abhijeet Praveen,Sareh Soleimani,Cormac Cureton,Aman Sidhu,Kintak Raymond Yu,Cristian Cojocaru,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用高速摄像机拍摄等离子体射流的视频数据来预测大气等离子喷涂（APS）过程中飞行粒子温度和速度的方法。通过提取视频特征并使用TabPFN、CNN等多种模型进行预测，发现基于视频的诊断方法具有良好效果，温度预测R²达0.90，速度预测R²达0.82。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APS过程中飞行粒子的温度和速度强烈影响涂层质量，但这些粒子特性难以在运行过程中连续监测，因此需要开发非侵入式的数据驱动诊断方法。

Method: (1) 从63次APS喷涂实验的126个喷涂前后视频记录中提取三种不同的视频衍生特征表示；(2) 使用TabPFN、CNN、随机森林、梯度提升、支持向量回归和XGBoost等模型进行预测；(3) 采用分组留一法交叉验证；(4) 评估基于原始视频帧的预训练CNN模型。

Result: (1) TabPFN在特征工程实验中表现最稳定，温度预测R²达0.86；(2) CNN在速度预测上表现更优，R²达0.81；(3) 预训练CNN直接处理原始视频帧效果最佳，温度和速度预测R²分别达0.90和0.82。

Conclusion: 视频衍生的等离子体射流信息为APS非侵入式诊断和实时过程监控提供了有前景且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Atmospheric plasma spraying (APS) is a widely used coating process in which in-flight particle temperature and velocity strongly influence coating quality. However, these particle characteristics are difficult to monitor continuously during operation, motivating the development of non-invasive data-driven diagnostic methods. In this work, we investigate the predictive potential of high-speed video observations of the plasma plume for estimating in-flight particle characteristics in APS. We introduce three different video-derived feature representations and evaluate them using Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Networks (TabPFN), convolutional neural networks (CNN), and classical regression baselines including Random Forest, Gradient Boosting, Support Vector Regression, and XGBoost. Experiments are conducted using grouped leave-one-out cross-validation on 126 labeled pre- and post-spray video recordings from 63 APS spray runs. Across the engineered feature experiments, TabPFN achieves the most consistent performance for temperature prediction, reaching R2 = 0.86 using the combined feature representation. CNN models particularly perform stronger for velocity prediction, achieving R2 of 0.81. In addition, we evaluate models operating directly on raw video frames using pretrained CNNs and find that the highest performance is achieved by a pretrained CNN with a regression head with R2 of 0.90 and 0.82 for temperature and velocity, respectively. The results demonstrate that video-derived plume information provides a promising and scalable foundation for non-invasive APS diagnostics and real-time process monitoring.

</details>


### [169] [Discovering Multiscale Deep Formulas in Complex Systems via Neural-Guided Lambda Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07426)
*Hanqiao Yu,Shusen Yang,Xuebin Ren,Cong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Deflex是一种端到端AI方法，结合可分解深度能量模型（Deflexformer）和Lambda演算符号回归模型（Deflexpressor），能够自动从多尺度复杂系统中提取不同形式的尺度特定公式（包括不变量和分布），在六个代表性系统上相比现有方法效率提升最高达7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于AI的方法在单尺度系统中表现良好，但难以从多尺度复杂系统中自动识别具有不同形式的尺度特定公式，亟需一种能够解耦多尺度潜在关系并发现多尺度公式的方法。

Method: Deflex由两个子系统组成：Deflexpressor（基于Lambda演算的符号回归模型，用于高阶公式）和Deflexformer（可分解深度能量模型，用于学习跨尺度的统一表示）。Deflexpressor生成合成数据预训练Deflexformer，后者通过解耦多尺度潜在关系来指导公式发现。

Result: 在六个具有不同行为的代表性复杂系统上，Deflex比现有最先进方法效率提升最高达7倍，并实现了自动化的多尺度发现。

Conclusion: Deflex有望成为跨学科科学发现的有用工具，能够自动从多尺度复杂系统中提取数学公式。

Abstract: A fundamental problem in science is identifying underlying patterns of complex systems in the form of concise mathematical formulas. Current Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based methods have shown strong performance in single-scale systems, yet remain limited in identifying scale-specific formulas in multiscale complex systems. We present Deflex, an end-to-end AI method to automatically extract multiscale formulas with potentially different forms, including invariants and distributions, from complex systems. Deflex consists of two subsystems named Deflexformer and Deflexpressor. Deflexpressor is a lambda-calculus symbolic regression model for higher-order formulas. Deflexformer is a decomposable deep energy model for learning unified representations across scales. Deflexpressor generates synthetic data to pre-train Deflexformer, which then guides formula discovery by decoupling multiscale latent relationships. Across six representative complex systems with diverse behaviors, Deflex achieves up to 7-fold higher efficiency than the state-of-the-art methods while enabling automated multiscale discovery. Our work could be a useful tool for scientific discovery across disciplines.

</details>


### [170] [Time series Foundation Models based on Physics-Informed Synthetic Histories for Cold-Start Photovoltaic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07457)
*Lorenzo Longarini,Alessandro Rongoni,Simone Silenzi,Emanuele Frontoni,Riccardo Rosati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种零样本冷启动光伏发电预测流程：利用植物元数据和气象协变量生成合成生产历史，使时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）能够通过推理时条件化进行预测。在440个PV站点、四个数据集上评估，协变量感知的基础模型表现优于基线约1.7-2倍（TabPFN-TS在真实反馈下MAE 0.514，Chronos-2在自预测反馈下最鲁棒），且性能对合成历史来源不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光伏电站在投产初期缺乏目标站点观测数据，无法直接使用标准监督预测方法，需要一种无需目标站点历史数据的零样本冷启动预测方案。

Method: 提出零样本流水线：从植物元数据和气象协变量生成合成生产历史，利用时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）通过推理时条件化进行预测。采用三种评估策略：严格冷启动基线、真实反馈和自预测反馈。

Result: 协变量感知的基础模型优于基线约1.7-2倍；TabPFN-TS在真实反馈下最优（MAE 0.514，RMSE 0.721 kWh kWp⁻¹ d⁻¹）；Chronos-2在自预测反馈下最鲁棒；性能对合成历史来源不敏感。

Conclusion: 零样本冷启动光伏预测可行且有效，精度主要取决于可用的合理时间上下文而非特定生成器，为实际部署提供了鲁棒方案。

Abstract: At commissioning time, Photovoltaic (PV) operators must forecast production before target-site observations are available, limiting the direct use of standard supervised forecasters. This cold-start setting is addressed with a zero-shot pipeline that generates a synthetic production history from plant metadata and meteorological covariates, enabling time-series foundation models (TSFMs) to forecast through inference-time conditioning. Five TSFMs are benchmarked against classical baselines under strict Cold-Start Baseline, Real Feedback, and Self-Forecast Feedback strategies. The evaluation spans $440$ PV sites across four datasets and diverse climate regimes. Covariate-aware foundation models outperform baselines by approximately $1.7-2\times$: TabPFN-TS achieves the lowest error under Real Feedback (MAE $0.514$, RMSE $0.721$ $kWh$ ${kWp}^{-1}$ ${d}^{-1}$), while Chronos-2 is most robust under Self-Forecast Feedback. Performance is largely insensitive to the synthetic-history source, indicating that accuracy is driven more by the availability of plausible temporal context than by the specific generator.

</details>


### [171] [Unsupervised Continual Clustering via Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07474)
*Mohammadreza Sadeghi,Sareh Soleimani,Zihan Wang,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无监督持续聚类（UCC）任务及前向-后向知识蒸馏方法FBCC，通过持续教师网络和轻量学生网络的双阶段蒸馏，在不存储旧数据的情况下保留历史聚类结构，有效缓解无监督持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督持续学习（UCL）方法忽视了聚类特定的目标，且传统缓解灾难性遗忘的策略（如知识蒸馏和重放缓冲区）存在内存和隐私问题。

Method: 提出FBCC（Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation for Continual Clustering），包含持续教师网络（带聚类投影头）和轻量任务特定学生网络，通过前向-后向双阶段蒸馏过程使教师学习新聚类的同时保留已发现的聚类结构，无需存储过往数据。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，FBCC在聚类准确率上持续优于现有持续学习基线，并显著减少了灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: FBCC作为UCC领域的开创性方法，有效解决了无监督持续学习中聚类目标缺失和记忆隐私问题，为无监督持续聚类提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Unsupervised Continual Learning (UCL) aims to enable neural networks to learn sequential tasks without labels or access to past data. A major challenge in this setting is Catastrophic Forgetting, where models forget previously learned tasks upon learning new ones. This challenge is amplified in UCL due to the absence of labels to guide learning and memory retention. Existing mitigation strategies, such as knowledge distillation and replay buffers, often raise memory and privacy concerns. Moreover, current UCL methods largely overlook clustering-specific objectives. To fill this gap, we introduce Unsupervised Continual Clustering (UCC) and propose Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation for Continual Clustering (FBCC). FBCC employs a continual teacher network with a clustering projector and lightweight task-specific students. Through a dual-phase forward-backward distillation process, the teacher learns new clusters while preserving previously discovered cluster structure without storing past data. FBCC represents a pioneering approach to UCC, demonstrating improved clustering performance across sequential tasks. Experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that FBCC consistently outperforms existing continual learning baselines in clustering accuracy while significantly reducing catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [172] [Graph Neural Network leveraging Higher-order Class Label Connectivity for Heterophilous Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07475)
*Takuto Takahashi,Itsuki Nakayama,Takahiro Mitani,Ryosuke Kikuchi,Yuya Sasaki,Makoto Onizuka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对GNN在异配图（heterophilous graphs）中节点分类性能受限的问题，本文提出标签上下文分类器（LCC），通过四种不同类型的游走生成标签上下文嵌入来捕获有向图中的高阶标签连接性，并可自适应地与任意GNN集成，实验表明集成LCC的GNN在异配有向图上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN在同配图中表现优异，但在异配图（不同标签节点更易相连）中性能有限，尤其无法捕获异配图中常见的高阶标签连接性。

Method: 提出Label Context Classifier (LCC)，通过四种不同类型的游走（walks）生成标签上下文嵌入（label context embeddings），利用这些嵌入估计目标节点的类别标签，并支持LCC与任意GNN的自适应重要性学习与集成。

Result: 集成LCC的GNN在异配有向图上的节点分类性能优于SOTA方法，标签上下文嵌入有效提升了分类准确率。

Conclusion: LCC通过捕获高阶标签连接性有效解决了异配图上的节点分类难题，且具有良好的通用性，可灵活与现有GNN架构结合。

Abstract: Node classification in graph neural networks (GNNs) has been widely applied in various fields of graph analysis. GNNs achieve high-accuracy node classification in homophilous graphs, where nodes with the same class label tend to be connected. However, their performance remains limited in heterophilous graphs, where nodes with different class labels are more likely to be connected. In particular, current GNNs derived from graph convolutional networks cannot capture higher-order class label connectivity, which is frequently observed in real-world heterophilous graphs. To address this issue, we propose a novel classifier, Label Context Classifier (LCC), designed to capture higher-order class label connectivity in directed graphs. LCC estimates the class label of a target node by leveraging label context embeddings that are generated through four distinct types of walks. In addition, our approach allows the integration of LCC and any GNN by adaptively learning their importance. Experimental results demonstrate that GNNs integrated with LCC outperform SOTA methods and the label context embeddings improve the node classification performance in heterophilous directed graphs.

</details>


### [173] [Drifting Models for Surrogate Flow Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07481)
*Chris R. Jung,Markus Dörr,Natalie Jüngling,Jennifer Niessner,Adam T. Müller,Nicolaj C. Stache*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件漂移（Conditional Drifting）的生成式替代模型，用于快速、高质量的单次室内流场生成。该方法在VAE潜空间中进行漂移，并采用标签感知掩码对齐边界条件，在精度和流一致性上媲美迭代扩散方法，同时推理速度加快两个数量级，并可通过空间条件扩展至未见几何形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFD虽能提供高保真流场，但计算成本高昂，限制了快速探索。现有生成式替代模型虽比确定性网络更好地建模分布，但迭代采样速度慢。需要一种既能保证生成质量又能实现单次快速推理的方法。

Method: 将生成式漂移框架适配到流体力学领域，构建条件架构：在学习的VAE潜空间中进行漂移，使用标签感知掩码机制将生成样本与边界条件对齐。同时提出空间条件变体，以推广到未见几何形状。

Result: 标签条件模型在精度和流一致性上匹配迭代扩散方法，同时推理速度提升两个数量级（约100倍）。空间条件变体为泛化到新几何形状提供了可行路径。

Conclusion: 条件漂移框架可作为扩散方法的高效替代方案，在推理速度至关重要的场景中实现实时CFD替代模型。

Abstract: While Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) provides high-fidelity flow fields for optimizing indoor environments, its computational cost limits rapid exploration. To solve this problem generative surrogates offer better distribution modeling than deterministic networks, but iterative sampling is slow. To enable high-quality, single-pass generation, we adapt the novel generative drifting framework to fluid mechanics. We introduce a conditional architecture that performs drifting in a learned VAE latent space and uses label-aware masking to align generated samples with their boundary conditions. Our label-conditioned model matches iterative diffusion in accuracy and flow consistency while running two orders of magnitude faster. Additionally, we propose a spatial-conditioning variant that establishes a promising path towards generalization to unseen geometries. Ultimately, conditional drifting serves as a highly efficient alternative to diffusion based approaches, unlocking real-time CFD surrogates where inference speed is critical.

</details>


### [174] [Network Recovery from Cascade Data: A Debiased Jacobian-Based Machine Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07483)
*Lei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CascadeNet，一种基于雅可比矩阵的机器学习框架，无需指定扩散模型即可从级联数据中恢复潜在影响网络，通过去偏雅可比估计实现$\sqrt{n}$一致性和渐近正态性，在模拟和真实COVID-19传播数据中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络恢复方法通常依赖于特定的扩散模型假设，当模型设定错误时性能严重下降。需要一种不预设扩散机制的网络恢复方法。

Method: 基于雅可比矩阵的机器学习框架：先构建一步转移函数的灵活估计器，再利用Neyman正交化去偏（通过Riesz表示子），得到去偏雅可比估计量，实现$\sqrt{n}$一致性和渐近正态性，支持网络结构的统计推断。

Result: 在9种常见数据生成过程的模拟中，CascadeNet的网络恢复准确率最高；在西班牙52省COVID-19传播的真实应用中，CascadeNet恢复的传播网络与真实省际流动网络显著相关，而基线方法无显著对齐。

Conclusion: CascadeNet是一种无需指定扩散模型的、具备统计推断能力的网络恢复方法，在多个数据生成过程中表现稳健且优于现有方法。

Abstract: Many important outcomes unfold as dynamic cascades, including product adoption, disease spread, financial distress, and information diffusion. A central challenge is to recover the hidden influence network behind these cascades. Existing methods typically assume a specific diffusion model, and their performance degrades substantially when that assumption is misspecified. We propose CascadeNet, a Jacobian-based machine learning framework for network recovery that does not require specifying a diffusion mechanism. The key idea is that the underlying influence structure can be characterized by the Jacobian of the one-step transition function. CascadeNet first constructs a flexible estimator of the transition function, and further applies Neyman-orthogonal debiasing via the Riesz representer, so that the debiased Jacobian is $\sqrt{n}$-consistent and asymptotically normal, enabling formal inference on the network structure. We validate CascadeNet in both a simulation exercise and a real-world empirical application. In simulations, where the data-generating process is known, CascadeNet achieves the highest network recovery accuracy across nine common data-generating processes. In an empirical application to COVID-19 transmission across Spain's 52 provinces, CascadeNet recovers transmission networks that are significantly correlated with the true inter-province mobility network, whereas networks recovered by baseline methods show no significant alignment with the ground truth.

</details>


### [175] [CoMetaPNS: Continually Meta-learning Personalized Neural Surrogates for Cardiac Electrophysiology Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07488)
*Ryan Missel,Xiajun Jiang,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种持续元学习框架，用于实现个性化心脏神经代理模型，能够在不遗忘旧知识的情况下持续整合新数据，并识别数据来源的动态类型，有效解决了传统方法在临床环境中需要完全重训练的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于少样本生成建模和元学习的个性化心脏代理模型假设训练分布静态且多样化，并需要已知任务标识符。当新数据顺序到达时，这些方法需要昂贵地使用所有先验数据进行重训练以避免灾难性遗忘，这在临床环境中不可行。

Method: 提出了一种持续元学习框架，利用基于记忆缓冲区的持续贝叶斯高斯混合模型来推断数据随时间变化的标识符和关系，从而实现个性化神经代理模型的持续学习。

Result: 在合成心脏数据上的实验表明，该方法在仿真预测、计算可扩展性和抗灾难性遗忘方面均优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 该持续元学习框架能够有效应对临床环境中顺序到达的未标记数据，实现持续集成信息并识别已知或未知动力学来源，同时避免灾难性遗忘。

Abstract: Personalized virtual heart simulations face challenges in model personalization and computational cost. While neural surrogates offer state-of-the-art solutions, they typically address either efficient personalization or training generalizable models. Recent work reframes this by learning the process of personalizing a surrogate using limited subject-specific context data, through few-shot generative modeling with set-conditioned surrogates and meta-learned amortized inference. These methods, however, assume a static and diverse training distribution with known task identifiers. When new data becomes available, they require costly retraining with all prior data to avoid catastrophic forgetting - a phenomena where the model forgets earlier tasks when trained on new ones. This is a major limitation in clinical settings where often unlabeled data arrives sequentially and full retraining is infeasible. This paper presents a new continual meta-learning framework to achieve personalized neural surrogates able to not only continually integrate information but also identify whether incoming data stems from a known or unknown dynamics source. By leveraging a continual Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Model over a memory buffer, our framework can infer the identifiers and relationships of data over time - required for effective meta-learning. Empirical results on synthetic cardiac data demonstrate superior simulation forecasting, computational scalability, and resilience to catastrophic forgetting compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [176] [Second-Order Path Kernel Interpolation Formulas in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07495)
*Jin Guo,Roy Y. He,Jean-Michel Morel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了神经网络的二阶路径核插值公式，在Domingos一阶理论基础上引入曲率加权项、随机梯度噪声耦合项和动量修正因子，并给出了预测浓度的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径核插值公式仅能一阶描述神经网络预测，缺乏对曲率、随机梯度噪声和动量等二阶效应的刻画，无法充分解释训练数据对预测的更精细影响机制。

Method: 推导了确定性梯度下降的二阶形式，引入曲率加权插值项；针对随机梯度下降，进一步耦合了小批量梯度噪声的协方差与预测曲率；扩展至带动量的SGD，引入记忆因子修正权重；并建立了预测浓度的集中估计。

Result: 获得了包含曲率项、噪声耦合项和动量修正因子的二阶路径核插值公式，以及围绕期望二阶表示的预测波动尺度界。

Conclusion: 这些结果细化了神经网络预测的路径核解释，为理解训练数据如何塑造神经网络预测提供了更精确的二阶理论框架。

Abstract: Understanding how training data shape neural network predictions is a central problem in modern learning theory. In 2020, Pedro Domingos proposed an interpolation formula valid for every model learned by deterministic gradient descent. It expresses the model's prediction as an integral, along the optimization path, of a data-dependent kernel that aligns the model's gradients at the test and training data. Such a first-order characterization remains valid for models trained with batch-based stochastic optimization. In this paper, we develop second-order forms of these interpolation formulas. We show that the leading path-kernel interpolation is supplemented by a curvature-weighted interpolation term. For stochastic gradient descent, an additional sampling-induced component appears, coupling the curvature of the prediction with the covariance of mini-batch gradient noise. We also extend the representation to stochastic gradient descent with momentum, where the interpolation structure is preserved but with the weights modified by a memory-related factor. Moreover, we establish a concentration estimate for the terminal prediction, identifying the fluctuation scale around the expected second-order representation. Together, these results provide a refinement of the path-kernel interpretation of neural network prediction.

</details>


### [177] [Accelerated Decentralized Stochastic Gradient Descent for Strongly Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07496)
*Ming Sun,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MG-ADSGD算法，通过结合Nesterov型原始-对偶外推与多轮快速gossip平均，首次在去中心化随机强凸优化中同时实现加速的√κ和1/√(1-β)依赖，取得了当前最优的通信复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化随机优化中，现有方法无法同时实现加速的条件数依赖√κ和网络谱间隙依赖1/√(1-β)，二者之间存在权衡未被解决。

Method: 提出MG-ADSGD算法，核心创新是将gossip通信轮数与mini-batch大小耦合，使得额外的通信轮次同时提升共识精度和降低梯度方差，结合Nesterov型原始-对偶外推技术。

Result: 实现了通信复杂度Õ(σ²/(μnε)·log(1/ε) + √(κ/(1-β))·log(1/ε))，据作者所知是当前去中心化随机强凸优化中已知最优的通信复杂度。

Conclusion: MG-ADSGD首次打破了去中心化随机优化中无法同时加速条件数和网络谱间隙的限制，为大规模分布式学习提供了更高效的通信方案。

Abstract: Decentralized stochastic optimization is a fundamental paradigm for large-scale learning over networks, where agents communicate only with their neighbors and no central coordinator is required. For strongly convex problems, communication efficiency is mainly determined by the condition number \(κ=L/μ\) and the network spectral gap \(1-β\). Although deterministic decentralized methods can simultaneously achieve accelerated \(\sqrtκ\) and \(1/\sqrt{1-β}\) dependences, no existing stochastic method attains both improvements at once. In this paper, we propose \emph{Multi-Gossip Accelerated DSGD} (MG-ADSGD), a decentralized stochastic algorithm that combines Nesterov-type primal--dual extrapolation with multi-round fast gossip averaging. The key idea is to couple the gossip depth with the mini-batch size so that additional communication rounds simultaneously improve consensus accuracy and reduce gradient variance. We show that MG-ADSGD achieves the communication complexity \[ \widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left( \frac{σ^2}{μnε}\log\frac{1}ε +
\sqrt{\fracκ{1-β}}\log\frac{1}ε \right), \] where \(ε\) denotes the target accuracy, \(n\) is the number of nodes, and \(σ^2\) is the gradient variance. To the best of our knowledge, this bound yields the best currently available communication complexity for decentralized stochastic strongly convex optimization, up to logarithmic factors that are independent of $ε$.

</details>


### [178] [Sparse Subspace-to-Expert Sharing for Task-Agnostic Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07500)
*Fatema Siddika,Md Anwar Hossen,Tanwi Mallick,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SETA提出了一种基于稀疏专家混合的自适应子空间分解方法，通过将知识分离为任务特定专家和共享专家，并引入自适应弹性锚定和路由感知正则化，解决了大语言模型持续学习中的可塑性-稳定性冲突，在LLaMA-2 7B和Qwen3-4B上实现了优异的知识保留与后向迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在持续学习中面临可塑性-稳定性困境：获取新能力时往往灾难性地遗忘先前知识。现有方法对参数进行统一处理，无法区分任务特定知识与共享能力，缺乏针对性的知识保护机制。

Method: 提出SETA框架，通过自适应稀疏子空间分解将知识分离为任务特定专家模块（捕捉任务特有模式）和共享专家模块（捕获通用特征）；采用自适应弹性锚定和路由感知正则化，在权重和路由两个层面联合保护共享知识；训练统一的门控网络在推理时自动检索正确的专家组合。

Result: 在多个领域特定基准上，SETA相比现有持续学习基线方法取得竞争性或更优的整体性能，尤其在早期任务知识保留和后向迁移方面表现突出，实验基于LLaMA-2 7B和Qwen3-4B。

Conclusion: SETA通过显式分离任务特定知识与共享知识，并引入多层保护机制，有效解决了持续学习中的可塑性-稳定性冲突，验证了专家混合架构在LLM持续学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Continual learning in Large Language Models (LLMs) is hindered by the plasticity-stability dilemma, where acquiring new capabilities often leads to catastrophic forgetting of previous knowledge. Existing methods typically treat parameters uniformly, failing to distinguish between specific task knowledge and shared capabilities. We introduce Mixture of Sparse Experts for Task Agnostic Continual Learning (SETA), a framework that resolves the plasticity-stability conflict through adaptive sparse subspace decomposition into task-specific expert modules. Unlike standard updates, where tasks compete for the same parameters, SETA separates knowledge into unique experts, designed to isolate task-specific patterns, and shared experts, responsible for capturing common features. This structure is maintained through adaptive elastic anchoring and a routing-aware regularization that jointly protect shared knowledge at both the weight and routing levels and enable a unified gating network to automatically retrieve the correct expert combination during inference. Extensive experiments across diverse domain-specific benchmarks demonstrate that SETA achieves competitive or superior overall performance relative to state-of-the-art continual learning baselines, with particularly strong retention of early-task knowledge and improved backward transfer on LLaMA-2 7B and Qwen3-4B.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Enhancing Malware Detection with Generative AI: Using Variational Autoencoders to Boost Machine Learning Classifiers' Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06501)
*Mohammad Alharbi,Jeremy Straub*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出使用变分自编码器（VAE）生成合成恶意软件样本，以缓解数据稀缺问题，并结合随机森林、XGBoost和序列模型等机器学习分类器进行训练，实验表明数据增强后分类器的准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数均显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件不断演进对网络安全构成挑战，尤其是新型或罕见恶意软件类型面临数据稀缺问题，亟需开发先进的检测技术来提升分类器的鲁棒性和适应性。

Method: 利用变分自编码器（VAE）生成高质量、多样化的合成恶意样本以增强数据集，将增强后的数据集分别用于训练随机森林、XGBoost和序列模型等机器学习分类器，并与原始数据训练的结果进行性能对比。

Result: 使用合成数据增强后的数据集训练分类器，在准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数上均取得显著提升，对各种恶意软件类别的检测性能均有改善。

Conclusion: 生成式AI（尤其是VAE）在网络安全领域具有实用价值，能够有效缓解数据稀缺问题、提升检测系统的鲁棒性，为未来构建更具弹性和适应性的恶意软件检测系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: The advancement of malware poses obstacles for cybersecurity, necessitating the development of advanced detection techniques. This paper proposes an approach to enhance malware detection through the use of a generative artificial intelligence model. Specifically, variational autoencoders (VAEs) are used with the random forest, XGBoost and sequential model machine learning classifiers. Generated synthetic malware samples are used to address the critical issue of data scarcity for new or less common malware types. This approach can be used to augment datasets to improve classifier robustness.
  The proposed methodology uses VAEs to create high-quality diverse synthetic datasets that closely mimic real-world malware data. The effectiveness of these augmented datasets is evaluated by comparing the performance of the machine learning classifiers when they are trained with the original data and when they are trained with the synthetic data-augmented datasets. The results demonstrate a notable improvement in the accuracy, precision, recall and F1-scores of the classifiers, when they are trained using the augmented datasets. The enhanced performance for detecting various malware classes shows the potential of this approach to facilitate adaptation to evolving malware threats effectively. This work demonstrates the utility of generative AI for cybersecurity. It also provides a foundation for future research aimed at developing more resilient and adaptive malware detection systems.

</details>


### [180] [Subtle Injection for Ground-truth Inference of LLM Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06502)
*Abraham Itzhak Weinberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SIGIL提出了一种通过在受保护文档中嵌入不可感知的触发序列（canary sequences），使训练在该文档上的LLM产生可统计检测的行为特征，从而实现训练数据成员推断的方法。在36,000次试验中，整体AUC达到0.892，即使面对100%释义攻击仍保持AUC>0.86。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在未经授权的情况下大量抓取网络语料进行训练，内容所有者需要一种法证学方法来证明其文档被包含在模型的训练集中，以维护版权和数据所有权。

Method: SIGIL框架包含五种注入策略（词汇稀有、词汇短语、句法、语义、代码模式），并基于Neyman-Pearson假设检验框架设计成员推断分数（MIS），通过模拟器参数校准实现正式的假阳性率控制。

Result: 在36,000次试验中，整体AUC=0.892，注入率从0.1%升至10%时AUC从0.831提升至0.947；代码模式策略AUC最高（0.903，d=1.84），句法结构最低（0.875，d=1.63）；即使100%释义攻击下仍保持AUC=0.864。

Conclusion: SIGIL通过嵌入不可感知触发序列实现了对LLM训练数据成员关系的高效、可控推断，且对表面级改写具有鲁棒性，为内容所有者提供了一种实用的法证学工具。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trained on scraped web corpora without authorisation, content owners require forensic methods to prove that their documents were included in a model's training set. We propose \textbf{SIGIL} (\textbf{S}ubtle \textbf{I}njection for \textbf{G}round-truth \textbf{I}nference of \textbf{L}LM training data), a framework that embeds imperceptible \emph{canary sequences} into protected text and code such that any LLM trained on those documents exhibits statistically detectable behavioural signatures when probed with targeted queries.
  SIGIL defines five canary strategies -- lexical-rare, lexical-phrase, syntactic, semantic, and code-pattern -- and a \emph{Membership Inference Score} (MIS) grounded in the Neyman-Pearson hypothesis testing framework with formal false-positive rate (FPR) control. Simulator parameters are calibrated against the empirical membership inference literature, yielding realistic heterogeneous results across $36{,}000$ trials: overall AUC $= 0.892$, rising from $0.831$ at $0.1\%$ injection to $0.947$ at $10\%$. Detection rates range from $33\%$ to $78\%$ across model-size and injection-rate conditions. Code Pattern canaries achieve the highest AUC ($0.903$, Cohen's $d = 1.84$); Syntactic Structure the lowest ($0.875$, $d = 1.63$). All four experimental factors -- injection rate, model size, canary strategy, and mixture ratio -- have significant independent effects on MIS ($p < 0.001$). SIGIL maintains AUC $> 0.86$ even under $100\%$ paraphrasing ($\text{AUC} = 0.864$), confirming robustness through semantic leakage that survives surface-level rewriting.

</details>


### [181] [MalTree: Tracing Malware Evolution from Embeddings at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06570)
*Akash Amalan,Georgios Smaragdakis,Tom J. Viering*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MalTree是一个受生物信息学启发的恶意软件系统发育分析框架，能够自动建模恶意软件家族间的进化关系，实现87%的时间一致性，并揭示了不同家族间高达10倍以上的进化速度差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统恶意软件检测是被动响应的——基于已知样本训练的模型随着威胁演变而性能下降。理解恶意软件家族间的进化关系有助于主动防御，但传统逆向工程需要数月乃至数年才能揭示谱系关系。

Method: 提出MalTree框架，应用生物信息学中的系统发育技术（UPGMA和邻接法），利用结构、行为和基于图像的特征自动建模恶意软件进化关系，并通过VirusTotal时间戳进行时间一致性验证。

Result: MalTree达到87%的时间一致性，发现某些家族变异速度比其他家族快10倍以上，Mirai僵尸网络案例研究证实推断的进化关系与已知威胁情报一致。

Conclusion: 该框架为恶意软件分析从逐样本分类向谱系感知的进化建模转变提供了基础，支持针对家族特定进化节奏定制检测策略。

Abstract: Malware detection remains largely reactive: machine learning models trained on known samples degrade as threats evolve. Understanding evolutionary relationships among malware families can inform proactive defense, but traditional reverse engineering can take months to years to uncover such lineage relationships. We propose MalTree, a framework that applies bioinformatics inspired phylogenetic techniques (UPGMA and Neighbor-Joining) at scale to model malware evolution automatically using structural, behavioral, and image-based features. We introduce temporal validation using VirusTotal timestamps to assess whether inferred trees reflect actual evolutionary order. MalTree achieves 87% temporal consistency, indicating that inferred evolutionary relationships closely align with real-world emergence timelines. Our analysis shows that some families mutate over 10 times faster than others, suggesting that detection strategies should be tailored to family-specific evolutionary tempos. Case studies, including the Mirai botnet, confirm that inferred relationships from our phylogenetic tree align with documented threat intelligence. Our framework provides a foundation for shifting malware analysis from sample-by-sample classification toward lineage-aware evolutionary modeling.

</details>


### [182] [AgileOS: A GPU Operating System Layer for Protected CUDA Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06697)
*Zhuoping Yang,Yiyu Shi,Alex Jones,Peipei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgileOS是一个GPU操作系统层，通过在库边界虚拟化CUDA，为受保护的GPU服务提供隔离机制，包括指针验证、内存访问保护和模块化服务架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU应用越来越多地与存储系统、网络设备、供应商库和GPU驻留服务交互，而非仅执行独立计算核函数。然而现有的CUDA编程模型默认将CUDA上下文、设备指针、运行时句柄等直接暴露给不可信的应用核函数，导致受保护的GPU服务需要自行构建临时的接口和隔离机制。

Method: AgileOS在库边界虚拟化CUDA：应用链接客户端CUDA Runtime/Driver/shim层，受信任的运行时工作者拥有真实CUDA上下文并仲裁操作。通过指针验证和PTX注入的内存访问守卫，将用户分配与受保护模块/MMIO范围分离。采用模块化架构，支持cuFFT、PyTorch等现有库。

Result: 原型实现了客户端拦截器、工作者端CUDA处理器、虚拟化CUDA对象表、受保护AgileOS模块、分离用户分配与受保护模块/MMIO范围的GPU内存管理器、选定的可信库适配器以及PTX级内核内存守卫。

Conclusion: AgileOS为GPU服务提供了一种操作系统级别的保护机制，通过虚拟化CUDA和内存隔离，在不改变现有编程模型的前提下增强安全性。

Abstract: Modern GPU applications increasingly interact with storage systems, network devices, vendor libraries, and GPU-resident services rather than executing only isolated compute kernels. This shift creates a need for operating-system-like protection around GPU services, where service metadata, device queues, memory-mapped I/O regions, and library-internal state should not be directly exposed to untrusted application kernels. However, today's CUDA programming model, by default, still gives each application direct ownership of its CUDA context, device pointers, runtime handles, module loading path, and kernel launches, leaving protected GPU services to build their own ad hoc interfaces and isolation mechanisms.
  This paper presents the initial design and prototype scope of AgileOS, a GPU operating-system layer for protected CUDA services. AgileOS virtualizes CUDA at the library boundary: applications link against client-side CUDA Runtime, Driver, and selected library shims, while a trusted runtime worker owns the real CUDA context and mediates supported operations. To protect service state and module interfaces, AgileOS also defines a GPU memory-management model that separates user allocations from protected module/MMIO ranges, using pointer validation and memory access guards via PTX injection. AgileOS is modularized and flexible, supporting a range of protected services and existing libraries such as cuFFT and PyTorch. The prototype includes client-side interceptors, worker-side CUDA handlers, virtualized CUDA object tables, protected AgileOS modules, a GPU memory manager that separates user allocations from protected module/MMIO ranges, selected trusted library adapters, and the PTX-level kernel memory guard.

</details>


### [183] [The Custody Envelope Threshold: Authority-Scaled Admission of External Artifacts in Institutional Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06767)
*Amadeus Brandes*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出**监管信封阈值（Custody Envelope Threshold）**，一种基于授权规模的人工制品准入模型，用于评估机构对外部依赖（包依赖、CI/CD动作、容器镜像、Terraform提供者、模型工件、MCP服务器等）的准入可辩护性，并通过四条件序数量表进行可操作化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基础设施高度依赖外部维护的工件（包注册依赖、CI/CD动作、容器镜像、Terraform提供者/模块、开发者扩展、模型工件、AI工具服务器等），这些工件易于获取但难以被机构正式准入、治理和撤销，缺乏系统化的准入风险评估框架。

Method: 提出监管信封阈值模型，以对象身份、入口路径和撤销能力的封闭性相对于工件被授予的执行权限来判断直接机构准入是否可辩护。当阈值未达到时，机构会采取代理、策略中介、供应商中介、内化、隔离或拒绝等替代策略。该框架通过四条件序数量表可操作化，并关联引用监控推理、最小权限原则和交易成本经济学。

Result: 将框架应用于包依赖、GitHub Actions、容器镜像、Terraform提供者/模块、开发者扩展和开放模型工件，并将MCP服务器作为保留证据进行验证。同时设计了验证方案、确定性预测函数和OSF复制包，用于检验高审查机构是否对高授权工件趋于更强的监管封闭性。

Conclusion: 监管信封阈值提供了一个系统化的、可验证的框架，帮助机构在面对日益复杂的外部依赖生态时，做出更严谨的工件准入决策，实现安全与效率的平衡。

Abstract: Modern infrastructure depends on externally maintained artifacts such as package-registry dependencies, CI/CD actions, container images, Terraform providers and modules, developer extensions, model artifacts, and AI tool servers. These artifacts are easy to fetch but difficult for institutions to admit, govern, and revoke. This paper proposes the Custody Envelope Threshold, an authority-scaled model of artifact admission. It argues that direct institutional admission is defensible only when object identity, ingress path, and revocation capacity are sufficiently closed relative to the execution authority delegated to the artifact. When this threshold is not met, institutions tend to proxy, policy-mediate, vendor-mediate, internalize, quarantine, or reject the artifact. The framework is operationalized as a four-condition ordinal instrument and connected to reference-monitor reasoning, least privilege, and transaction cost economics. It is applied to package dependencies, GitHub Actions, container images, Terraform providers and modules, developer extensions, and open model artifacts, with Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers treated as held-out evidence. The paper also specifies a validation design, deterministic prediction function, and OSF replication package for testing whether high-scrutiny institutions converge toward stronger custody closure for high-authority artifacts.

</details>


### [184] [What Your Posts Reveal: A Benchmark and Agentic Framework for User-Level Privacy Leakage on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06784)
*Zifan Peng,Yini Huang,Aiwen Lu,Qiming Ye,Peixian Zhang,Jingyi Zheng,Yule Liu,Xuechao Wang,Xinlei He,Jiaheng Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SopriBench基准和隐私暴露评分PES，并设计Argus框架用于跨帖子累积隐私泄露推理，在合成数据上实现25%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏统一的用户级多模态隐私泄露基准，以及能够捕捉暴露严重程度（超越二元准确性）的评估指标。

Method: 构建SopriBench合成基准（50个用户画像、1,569张图片），提出PES指标（基于上下文敏感性和粒度加权），设计Argus推理框架（基于溯因推理、假设形成与证据验证）。

Result: Argus达到0.55 PES，相比最强基线提升25%，在跨帖子泄露场景中增益最大。

Conclusion: Argus能有效聚合跨帖子线索进行累积隐私推断，PES可作为更细粒度的隐私暴露评估指标。

Abstract: Public social media posts can reveal private information through weak cues scattered across text, images, or metadata. Such leakage is often cumulative and cross-post: cues that appear harmless in isolation may jointly expose a user's home, workplace, or routine. However, current research lacks a unified benchmark for user-level multimodal privacy leakage and an evaluation metric that captures exposure severity beyond binary accuracy.
  To address these gaps, we propose SopriBench, a synthetic benchmark guided by leakage patterns abstracted from a private reference corpus of Rednote and Instagram accounts, covering 50 user profiles and 1,569 images with attributes, contextual sensitivity, granularity, leakage type, inference difficulty, and supporting evidence. We further introduce the Privacy Exposure Score (PES), which weights value granularity by contextual sensitivity. Inspired by abductive reasoning, we introduce Argus, a training-free agentic framework for cumulative leakage inference. Argus forms hypotheses from accumulated evidence, verifies supporting evidence, and aggregates cross-post cues into privacy profiles, achieving 0.55 PES, a 25% improvement over the strongest baseline, with the largest gain on cross-post leakage.

</details>


### [185] [AMD-FCG: An Enhanced Function Call Graph Dataset with Integrated Topological Features for Malware Detection and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06815)
*Parthajit Borah,Sakshi Singh,D. K. Bhattacharyya,J. K. Kalita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了AMD-FCG，一个增强型函数调用图数据集，通过整合恶意软件的拓扑特征来提升检测效率，无需动态分析和大量处理即可支持高效的恶意软件检测系统开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件结构复杂、行为多样，传统检测方法难以有效应对，亟需一种可靠且自适应的解决方案，能够基于大规模数据集分析恶意软件的结构和行为模式。

Method: 构建增强型函数调用图（FCG）数据集AMD-FCG，整合恶意软件的拓扑特征，覆盖多种恶意软件家族及良性应用样本，无需动态分析即可完成检测。

Result: AMD-FCG数据集能够有效覆盖多种恶意软件家族，提升检测流程的准确性和鲁棒性，简化网络安全专业人员的工作流程。

Conclusion: AMD-FCG为开发更高效、创新的恶意软件检测系统提供了可靠的数据基础，消除了对动态分析和大量预处理的需求。

Abstract: As malware illustrates a complex structure and behavior, detection of these has been a significant challenge in the domain of cybersecurity along with related services in daily life. So, it becomes crucial to have a reliable and adaptive solution to address the issue. Among the several detection methods developed over the years, one of the most reliable ones is studying and analyzing the structural and behavioral patterns of malware. These patterns of sophisticated malware can be obtained with the help of Function Call Graphs (FCGs). However, to effectively cover numerous groups of families of malware, it is required to have a sufficiently large dataset for the system to operate on. In order to ensure accuracy and robustness of the system, the dataset should comprise samples of different malwares and a benign application for secure execution of the detection process. This paper introduces AMD-FCG, an enhanced Function Call Graph dataset integrated with topological features of malwares. The framework enhances the detection procedure, streamlining the workflow for cybersecurity professionals and also eliminating the need for dynamic analysis and extensive processing. Therefore, it can be used to develop and deploy more efficient and innovative malware detection systems.

</details>


### [186] [On the Incentive Compatibility of Block Propagation in Bitcoin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06860)
*Fumichika Maeda,Akira Sakurai,Taishi Nakai,Kazuyuki Shudo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过建模分析发现，比特币矿工缺乏传播他人区块的奖励激励，而不同 tie-breaking 规则在传播激励与挖矿公平性之间存在权衡：优先看见规则提供最强传播激励但公平性最差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽已关注传播延迟对分叉和挖矿收益的影响，但尚未完整刻画不同 tie-breaking 规则下矿工改善区块传播的激励，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于区块链网络模型，推导不同 tie-breaking 规则下的挖矿奖励解析表达式，显式刻画传播延迟、算力分布和 tie-breaking 规则对挖矿收益的联合影响，并据此分析矿工的传播激励。

Result: 矿工没有传播他人区块的奖励激励；在优先看见规则下，每个非多数派矿工都有激励更快接收和传播自己的区块；不同 tie-breaking 规则存在权衡：优先看见规则提供最强传播激励但公平性最差。

Conclusion: 比特币的激励设计存在缺陷——矿工缺乏传播他人区块的动机，且 tie-breaking 规则的选择必须在传播激励与挖矿公平性之间做出取舍。

Abstract: Bitcoin is permissionless and does not rely on any central administrator, which gives it strong censorship resistance. At the same time, it is important to incentivize miners to behave in ways that align with the interests of the system as a whole. This paper asks whether miners are individually incentivized to propagate blocks, one of the most fundamental processes in Bitcoin. Miners collectively maintain the blockchain by generating blocks and disseminating them across the network. If miners have an incentive not to propagate some blocks, this would indicate a fundamental flaw in Bitcoin's incentive design. Although prior work has studied how propagation delays affect forks and mining rewards, it has not fully characterized miners' incentives to improve block propagation under different tie-breaking rules. To address this gap, we derive analytical reward expressions for each tie-breaking rule based on a blockchain network model that captures the effect of forks on mining fairness. These expressions explicitly characterize how block propagation delays, hashrate distribution, and tie-breaking rules jointly determine mining rewards. We then use them to analyze miners' incentives to improve block propagation. Our results show, for example, that miners have no mining-reward incentive to relay blocks generated by other miners. By contrast, under the first-seen rule, every non-majority miner is incentivized to receive other miners' blocks more quickly and to propagate its own blocks more quickly. Finally, we compare tie-breaking rules and identify a trade-off between propagation incentives and mining fairness. In particular, the first-seen rule provides the strongest incentives to reduce propagation delays, but it also worsens mining fairness the most.

</details>


### [187] [FDM: A Framework for Decision-making to build ML-based Malware detection systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06894)
*Tadiwa Vhito,Jakapan Suaboot,Warodom Werapun,Norrathep Rattanavipanon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出框架FDM，通过加权配置兼容性评分(WCCS)在五个操作约束下为恶意软件检测推荐最优ML配置。实验表明最优ML方案具有上下文依赖性：XGBoost在二分类中表现最佳，而经典模型在多分类中优于深度模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择适用于恶意软件检测的ML配置是一个复杂的多准则问题，模型选择、特征工程和更新机制必须共同满足不同部署环境下的操作约束，现有方法缺乏系统化的决策框架。

Method: 提出FDM框架，使用WCCS多准则评分函数，将五个操作参数（平台约束、资源预算、响应延迟、更新频率、检测灵敏度）映射到九个配置维度的排序推荐。在三个数据集上进行四项实验验证。

Result: (i) XGBoost二分类准确率97.46%，RAM<70MB，优于LSTM/BiLSTM（高达2.8GB）；(ii) 多分类中XGBoost(79.03%)优于BiLSTM(72.27%)，排序反转；(iii) 增量学习EfficientNetB0保持99.13%准确率，仅降0.65pp；(iv) 迁移学习训练提速2.14倍；(v) 自编码器预处理训练提速14倍，准确率仅降0.86pp。

Conclusion: 最优ML配置具有上下文依赖性，验证了FDM框架的核心前提及其对网络安全从业者的实用价值。

Abstract: Selecting appropriate machine learning (ML) configurations for malware detection is a complex, multi-criteria problem. Model choice, feature engineering, and update mechanisms must jointly satisfy operational constraints that vary across deployment contexts. This paper proposes the Framework for Decision-making (FDM) to build ML-based malware detection systems. The FDM formalises this selection process using the Weighted Configuration Compatibility Score (WCCS), a multi-criteria scoring function mapping five operational parameters (platform constraint, resource budget, response latency, update frequency, and detection sensitivity) to ranked recommendations across nine configuration dimensions. To validate the framework, four experiments were conducted on three datasets (a private Windows API dataset, the public Malimg image benchmark, and an Android static API dataset). Key results include: (i) XGBoost achieved the best accuracy-to-resource ratio in binary classification (97.46 % test accuracy, <70 MB RAM), outperforming LSTM/BiLSTM which consumed up to 2.8 GB; (ii) in multi-class classification, classical models (XGBoost 79.03 %) outperformed recurrent deep models (BiLSTM 72.27 %), reversing the binary ranking; (iii) class-incremental learning with EfficientNetB0 maintained 99.13 % accuracy with only 0.65 pp degradation across 11 incremental steps; (iv) transfer learning reduced training time by 2.14 times on average for image-based malware data without significant accuracy cost; and (v) autoencoder pre-processing yielded a 14 times training speedup at a cost of only 0.86 pp accuracy. These findings confirm that the optimal ML configuration is context-dependent, validating the FDM's core premise and demonstrating its practical utility for cybersecurity practitioners.

</details>


### [188] [Blockchain Infrastructure for Intelligent Cyber--Physical--Social Systems:Post-Quantum Security, Interoperability, and Trustworthy Data Economies in the Era of Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06895)
*Song Guo,Huawei Huang,Dongping Liu,Aoyu Zhang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本教程探讨了将区块链作为协调层，在体现代理人工智能（世界模型机器人）与量子计算威胁的双重转型中，构建抗量子密码学、可扩展跨分片架构及可信数据经济的基础设施，并包含AWS Braket量子硬件演示与开源框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体现代理AI的部署对区块链基础设施提出了可信数据溯源、跨组织治理和激励兼容共享的迫切需求；同时，量子计算进展威胁现有密码学原语，需要抗量子敏捷架构来保障长期可验证性。

Method: 通过五个集成模块展开：体现代理AI与世界模型需求→量子硬件现实与证据驱动的安全迁移→BrokerChain跨分片架构→基于Croissant元数据标准与机器人学习溯源的可信数据经济→多模态云部署的产业生态集成，并以AWS Braket量子硬件演示开场。

Result: 建立了区块链从金融底层向网络-物理-社会系统基础设施转型的路线图，提供了抗量子、可互操作、数据可信的开放框架，并展示了ECDSA到后量子签名的过渡实践。

Conclusion: 区块链可作为统一基础设施，桥接量子硬件现实与体现代理AI数据需求，支撑下一代去中心化智能环境。

Abstract: The deployment of embodied artificial intelligence via world-model-based robotics presents a transformative opportunity for blockchain infrastructure, establishing urgent demand for trustworthy data provenance, cross-organizational governance, and incentive-compatible sharing across decentralized ecosystems. Simultaneously, quantum computing advances recognized by the 2025 Nobel Prize in Physics and the Turing Award threaten the cryptographic primitives securing these data economies, creating an interdependent imperative: long-lived verification for embodied AI depends on crypto-agile architectures capable of withstanding quantum adversaries. This tutorial examines blockchain as the coordination layer bridging this dual transition, from financial substrate to foundational Cyber-Physical-Social Systems infrastructure that simultaneously secures against quantum cryptanalysis and enables scalable, trustworthy data economies. The session opens with an immersive AWS Braket demonstration engaging participants with superconducting, trapped-ion, and neutral-atom hardware to assess cryptographic threat timelines and witness ECDSA-to-post-quantum signature transitions. Five integrated modules progress from embodied AI and world-model requirements through quantum hardware reality and evidence-based security migration, to scalable cross-shard architectures via BrokerChain protocols, trustworthy data economies implementing Croissant metadata standards and robotic learning provenance, and industry ecosystem integration for multi-modal cloud deployment. By bridging quantum hardware realities with embodied AI data requirements, this tutorial charts blockchain as unified infrastructure for next-generation decentralized intelligent environments, providing open-source frameworks and roadmaps for architecting quantum-resistant, interoperable, and data-trustworthy systems.

</details>


### [189] [DPAgent-in-the-Middle: Agentic Defense and Repair Against AI-Groomed Deceptive Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06914)
*Zewei Shi,Ruoxi Sun,Haoyang Li,Seong Oun Hwang,Feng Liu,Minhui Xue,Xingliang Yuan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DPAgent的智能体框架，通过潜在空间净化与防御性提示相结合的方式，主动检测、探索和修复网页中的隐私欺骗模式，以应对AI诱导（AI Grooming）攻击。实验表明，DPAgent在检测率（90.98%）、模式覆盖率（>80%）、页面探索效率（仅需约10%的页面）和修复成功率（77%）上均表现优异，大规模网站研究发现高达98%的网站存在隐私欺骗模式，其中90%以上可被DPAgent缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对网页界面隐私欺骗模式的防御手段存在碎片化、静态化的问题，且易受大语言模型操纵；同时，数据空洞（data voids）为攻击者提供了注入误导性内容的温床，进而放大欺骗性设计和模型行为异常。因此，亟需一种主动、动态且能对抗新兴AI威胁的防御机制。

Method: 1) 形式化定义了AI Grooming威胁模型，即攻击者利用数据空洞植入看似良性但恶意的样本，以污染模型推理并正常化欺骗行为。2) 提出DPAgent框架，包含四个专业化智能体，通过潜在空间净化（latent space purification）与防御性提示（defensive prompting）相结合的方式，在真实网页环境中主动探索、检测和修复隐私欺骗用户界面。

Result: DPAgent检测出90.98%的被诱导样本，在隐私欺骗模式检测上达到最先进的微F1分数0.816；仅需基线方法约10%的页面即可探索超过80%的模式类型；成功修复77%被检测到的欺骗界面。大规模研究发现485个网站中高达98%存在至少一个隐私欺骗模式，其中超过90%可被DPAgent缓解。用户研究证实DPAgent能有效降低隐私风险且不损害浏览体验。

Conclusion: DPAgent展示了智能体介入式防御（agent-in-the-middle）在保护网页UI供应链免受欺骗性设计和基于数据空洞利用的新兴AI威胁方面的潜力，为应对AI Grooming攻击提供了一种主动、高效且可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Privacy deceptive patterns in web interfaces systematically manipulate users into disclosing personal data, yet existing defenses are fragmented, static, and increasingly vulnerable to manipulation by large language models. Moreover, data voids, areas of information scarcity within the web ecosystem, create fertile ground for adversaries to inject misleading content that can be scraped and learned by AI systems, thereby amplifying both deceptive design and model misbehavior. In this paper, we formalize a new threat model, AI grooming, where attackers exploit data voids to seed benign-looking but malicious samples that corrupt model reasoning and normalize deceptive practices. To address this threat in privacy deceptive patterns, we present DPAgent, an agentic and reasoning-aware framework that orchestrates four specialized agents to mitigate the AI Grooming threat via a proactive defense that combines latent space purification with defensive prompting and operates directly in live web environments to proactively explore, detect, and repair privacy deceptive user interfaces before they reach end users. Extensive evaluations show that DPAgent detects 90.98% of groomed samples, achieves state-of-the-art privacy deceptive pattern detection with a micro F1 of 0.816, explores over 80% of pattern types while visiting only about 10% of the pages required by baselines, and successfully repairs 77% of detected deceptive interfaces. A large-scale study of 485 websites in the wild reveals that up to 98% contain at least one privacy deceptive pattern, over 90% of which can be mitigated by DPAgent. User studies further confirm that DPAgent effectively reduces privacy risks while preserving browsing experience. Our results demonstrate the promise of agent-in-the-middle defenses for securing the web UI supply chain against deceptive design and emerging AI threats rooted in data void exploitation.

</details>


### [190] [HAVE: Host Active Verification Engine for Closing the Contextual Reality Gap in Security Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06968)
*Vincenzo Sammartino,Marco Pasquini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: HAVE通过主机主动验证引擎，利用快照隔离的伯努利试验进行最大似然估计，测量经验性入侵概率$\hat{p}$，并通过Wilson区间置信权重$\alpha_w$结合贝叶斯混合规则修正CVSS评分导致的上下文现实差距，在四个漏洞类别中实现$P_{\text{reach}}$平均+124.1%的净修正，并将校准指数波动从4.6倍降至1.12倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全数字孪生(SDT)依赖理论CVSS分数分配横向移动概率，导致上下文现实差距：未被承认的缓解措施使风险被高估，而绕过内存安全防御的逻辑缺陷使风险被严重低估。

Method: 提出HAVE扩展，部署安全受限的主机代理，通过快照隔离的伯努利试验进行最大似然估计测量经验性入侵概率$\hat{p}$；使用Wilson区间宽度置信权重$\alpha_w$，通过贝叶斯混合规则（形式上与Beta-Binomial后验相关）将$\hat{p}$传播到蒙特卡洛模拟中。

Result: 在四个漏洞类别、三个安全层级和两个生产二进制文件上，HAVE在假阳性场景中降低$P_{\text{reach}}$ 38.2%，在假阴性场景中提高132.4%，净修正+124.1%；校准后HAVE估计在不同指数$\kappa$下仅变化1.12倍，而CVSS基线变化4.6倍。

Conclusion: HAVE通过经验性测量有效弥合了SDT中的上下文现实差距，显著提升了横向移动风险评估的准确性，在不同校准条件下均表现出远优于CVSS基线的稳定性和一致性。

Abstract: Security Digital Twins (SDTs) provide continuously updated virtual replicas of infrastructure for threat simulation, yet they rely on theoretical CVSS scores to assign lateral-movement probabilities -- creating the Contextual Reality Gap: risk is overestimated where unacknowledged mitigations neutralize exploits, and drastically underestimated where logic flaws bypass all memory-safety defenses. We present the Host Active Verification Engine (HAVE), an SDT extension that deploys a safety-constrained host agent to measure the empirical probability of compromise $\hat{p}$ via maximum-likelihood estimation over snapshot-isolated Bernoulli trials. A Wilson interval-width confidence weight $α_w$ propagates $\hat{p}$ into Monte Carlo simulations via a Bayesian blending rule formally related to the Beta-Binomial posterior. Evaluation across four vulnerability classes, three security tiers, and two production binaries shows HAVE reduces $P_{\text{reach}}$ by 38.2% in false-positive scenarios and increases it by 132.4% in false-negative scenarios, with a net +124.1% correction; post-HAVE estimates vary by only $1.12\times$ across calibration exponents $κ$, versus $4.6\times$ for CVSS-only baselines.

</details>


### [191] [The Sound of Malware: A Memory Forensics Approach for Android Malware Analysis via Audio Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07005)
*Silvia Lucia Sanna,Massimo Palozzi,Leonardo Regano,Riccardo Lazzeretti,Giorgio Giacinto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于内存取证和信号处理的恶意软件检测框架\approachname，将Android应用的静态字节码和早期执行内存快照转换为音频波形，利用谱特征、CNN和Transformer进行恶意软件分类，在CICMalDroid2020和VirusTotal数据集上达到98.0%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Android恶意软件分析方法面临代码混淆、动态加载、加壳及隐写攻击等高级逃逸策略的挑战，基于签名的系统和依赖显式语义指标（如权限、API调用、控制流结构）的机器学习模型可靠性下降。

Method: 提出基于内存取证和信号分析的框架\approachname，将静态字节码和早期执行内存快照通过直接二进制到波形映射转化为音频信号，保留底层结构模式，无需反汇编或特征工程；使用手工设计的谱描述符、CNN和Transformer嵌入进行信号处理与分类。

Result: 在CICMalDroid2020数据集和VirusTotal恶意软件上，\approachname达到98.0%准确率，优于静态声波化方法和当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 将分析视角从语义程序建模转向基于信号的底层结构表征，通过二进制到音频波形的映射和深度学习谱分析，可有效应对高级逃逸攻击，实现高精度恶意软件检测。

Abstract: Android malware analysis is currently facing increasing challenges in achieving robust classification and detecting stealth attacks. Modern threats employ advanced evasion strategies such as code obfuscation, dynamic loading, packing, and even steganographic manipulation of traditional static and dynamic features. These techniques reduce the effectiveness of signature-based systems and degrade the reliability of Machine Learning models that depend on explicit semantic indicators such as permissions, API calls, or control-flow structures. In this work, we propose \approachname, a memory forensics malware detection framework that shifts the analysis perspective from semantic program modeling to signal-based structural representation. Both static bytecode and early-execution memory snapshots are transformed into audio waveforms through direct binary-to-waveform mapping, preserving low-level structural patterns without requiring disassembly or feature engineering. The resulting signals are processed using handcrafted spectral descriptors, Convolutional Neural Networks, and transformer-based embeddings. Experiments on CICMalDroid2020 dataset and VirusTotal malware demonstrate that \approachname achieves up to 98.0\% accuracy, outperforming static sonification and competitive state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [192] [Fast Bounded-Independence Functions and Their Duals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07009)
*Martijn Brehm,Yuval Ishai,Nicolas Resch*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了可用线性规模电路计算的快速函数，在密码学应用驱动下，构建了同时优化电路规模和代数度的快速t-wise独立哈希函数、改进的快速编码族（达到Gilbert-Varshamov界，失败概率可忽略，支持一般域和速率、系统编码及快速通用编码器），以及支持最优组合列表解码的快速线性函数族。这些成果首次实现了电路复杂度随参与者数量线性扩展的完美安全多方计算协议，以及具有最优渐近电路复杂度的加密矩阵-向量乘积计算协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码学应用需要具备随机函数特性的快速函数（可由线性规模电路计算），现有研究在t-wise独立哈希函数、快速编码族以及更强随机性质方面存在不足，尤其是缺乏同时优化电路规模和代数度的构造，以及支持组合列表解码等更强性质的快速线性函数族。

Method: 通过代数构造方法，基于有限域F2上的代数结构设计快速t-wise独立哈希函数；改进并简化ITCS 2026的编码构造，降低失败概率并扩展至一般域和速率；构造快速线性函数族，将t个线性独立输入映射到均匀统计独立的输出，并证明其支持最优组合列表解码。

Result: 构建了代数度为log2 t的快速t-wise独立哈希函数（同时优化电路规模和度）；改进了快速编码族构造，失败概率可忽略，支持一般域/速率、系统编码和快速通用编码器；构建了支持最优组合列表解码的快速线性函数族（此前仅t=1已知）；基于上述结果实现了首个电路复杂度线性于参与者数量的完美安全MPC协议，以及最优渐近复杂度的加密矩阵-向量乘积协议。

Conclusion: 本文在快速函数构造方面取得了系统性进展，填补了多项理论空白，其成果在密码学中具有重要应用价值，尤其在完美安全多方计算和加密计算领域实现了电路复杂度的最优渐近性能。

Abstract: We continue the study of {\em fast} functions, computable by linear-size circuits, that share useful properties of random functions. Motivated by cryptographic applications, we generalize and improve on previous results in this area, obtaining the following results:
